cekku ural ūṭē irukka ataṉ cuṟṟu virivu peritu. cekkic cuṟṟi virintu cellum parappik koṇṭu ataṟku aminta peyar parañcam eṉpatu. nīril tōṉṟi viriyum nurikkup parañcam eṉṉum peyaruṇṭu.

paraṭṭi:
tali cīvāmal eṇṇey tēykkāmal iruntāl talimuṭi paṭiyāmal eḻumpik kiṭakkum. ataṉip paraṭṭi eṉpar. paraṭṭit tali taṉ aḷavil paravi virintu kāṭci aḷittal veḷippaṭi. avvāṟē ili kuḻi kavintu iṟaṅkāmal mēlēyum pakkattum virintu tōṉṟum maram paraṭṭi maram eṉappaṭutal vaḻakku. moṭṭikkum paraṭṭikkum muraṇutal nili. paraṭṭi eṉpataṟkuc ceṭi mutaliyaṉa tali parantu niṟki eṉṉum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarāti.
paraṭṭayam:
paraṭṭayam eṉpatōr uṭi vakiyic cūḷāmaṇi cuṭṭukiṟatu. ataṟku oṭṭuc callaṭam eṉak kuṟippuri yuḷatu.
oṭṭiya kaliṅkam tāḷmēl tirittuṭut turuvak kōṭip
paṭṭiki patippa yāttup paraṭṭayam narala vīkkip
kaṭṭiya kaḻalar
eṉpatu appāṭṭu (842). narala vīkki eṉṟatāl olikkumāṟu iṟukkak kaṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa varum. atu kaḻalukkuk kīḻē paraṭṭil oli kiḷarumāṟu aṇiyappaṭum ōraṇi eṉak koḷḷalāmō eṉa eṇṇa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
paraṭṭik kīri eṉpatoru kīriyip patārttakuṇa cintāmaṇi (603) kūṟukiṟatu. ataṉ amippik karutiya peyarāka irukkalām.
paraṭu:
kāliṉ oru pakutikkup paraṭu eṉpatu peyar. atu karaṇṭi eṉappaṭum. kaṇukkālukkuk kīḻē paṭarntu viriyum aṭiyiṉ mēlpakutiyē paraṭām. itaṉip paṭam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkāl ataṉ akalam aṟippaṭum. paṭattit tēyttuk kaḻuvu; evvaḷavu aḻukku, pār eṉpatu kuḻantikaḷik kuḷikka vikkum tāymār uri.
iṭicceṟi kuṟaṅku kuvik kimpuri iḷaka miṉṉum
puṭicciṟu paraṭu pullik kiṇkiṇi cilampō ṭārppa
eṉpatu cintāmaṇi (2445).

paraṭaṉ:
ōriṭattu nilittu vēli ceyya vāykkāmal palappalariṭam palappala iṭaṅkaḷil appoḻutik kappoḻutu vēli ceyyum kūliyāḷaṉ paraṭaṉ eṉappaṭutal avaṉ toḻilnili karutiya peyarām.
paraṇ, paraṇi:
kāṭṭuk kāvalukkākak kālkaḷ ūṉṟik kampukaḷip parattic ceyyappaṭum kāval mēṭi paraṇ eṉappaṭum. paraṇi eṉpatum atuvē. kūri vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvar maṭṭattil kampukaḷip parappip palakiyaṭippuc ceytu paraṇ ākkuvatum uṇṭu. atil paḻam poruḷkaḷ, viṟaku mutaliyavaṟṟip pōṭṭu vippatu vaḻakkam.
paraṇkaḷi marattiṉmēl amikkum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu. marakkiḷikaḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟik kaḻikaḷālum kuccikaḷālum iṇittup paṭal parappi vippar. itu perumpālum kāṭṭiṉ aṭivāraṅkaḷil amikkappaṭum. ivvaḻakkam paṇṭē uṇṭu eṉpatu,
kalikeḻu maramicic cēṇōṉ iḻitta
puliyañ citaṇam
eṉak kuṟiñcip pāṭṭil (40, 41) collappaṭukiṟatu. itaṇam eṉpatu paraṇ.
kalikeḻu mīmicic cēṇōṉ
eṉa varum cilampukku (25:30)c cēṇōṉ = paraṇiṉ mēlōṉ eṉa arumpata vurikārar poruḷ eḻutiyami aṟiyattakkatu.
itaṇ eṉap paraṇ kuṟikkappaṭṭālum kampar,
toṭar mañcam pōluḷa paraṇ
eṉa āḷkiṟār (ayōt. 673) iṉip paraṇam eṉpatum paraṇik kuṟippatēyām.
paraṇṭutal:
kaḷi kuttutal, kaḷi kiḷḷutal, kaḷi eṭuttal, kaḷi paṟittal, kaḷi veṭṭal iṉṉavāka vaḻaṅkum oruviṉip pala kūṟukaḷuḷ kaḷi paraṇṭal eṉpatum oṉṟu. ataṟkuriya karuvi kaḷiparaṇṭi. kaḷi curaṇṭiyiṉum virinta takaṭuṭiyatu kaḷiparaṇṭi. kaḷikotti iṭapparappic curukki āḻntu cellum. kaḷiparaṇṭi iṭapparappi virittu, mēlāl taṭavic cellum. kaḷi curaṇṭiyum mēlāl nilatti vaḻittuc celvatē. eṉiṉum, paraṇṭiyiṉ aḷavukkuk kuṟintatu. eli tuḷittal, ōriṭattut tōṇṭutal; eli paraṇṭal akalamākak kiḷittu eṟital. paraṇṭutalukku virital poruḷ uṭimi ivaṟṟāl viḷaṅkum.
paraṇṭi:
paraṭu eṉṉum poruḷ tarum col paraṇṭi eṉpatām. paraṇṭi eṉa oru paṟavi uṇṭeṉac carvārtta ciṟpa cintāmaṇi eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṟatu (16).
paraṇṭi valattiṟ pāṭi valattirun tiṭattiṟ pōntu
eṉpatu atu. irupālum ciṟaku piṟapaṟavikaḷiṉum virintiyalum taṉmiyāl ippaṟavikku ippeyar ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. aṉṟip paraṇṭutal uṭimiyālum peyar peṟṟirukkalām. cila paṟavikaḷ kāṭṭuc ceṭikaḷ tūṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippakutiyil paraṇṭik kuḻiyākki muṭṭiyiṭṭu aṭikāttuk kuñcu porittal uṇṭu eṉpati aṟiyiṉ ivvūkam koḷḷattakkatām.
paraṇacāli:
paraṇ, paraṇi eṉpavaṟṟi muṉṉē aṟivōm. apparaṇ, ūṉṟiya kālkaḷiṉ mēl allatu marakkiḷiyiṉ mēl amikkappaṭṭavi. ip paraṇacāliyō, kālkaḷi nērkālkaḷākavō, mukaṭu kūṭṭutalākavō ūṉṟi, ili taḻi puṟkaḷāl vēyntu vikkappaṭuvatāl - kuccikaḷi ūṉṟic ceyyappaṭuvatāl kuccil eṉappaṭum.
pūppaṭintāḷukkeṉat taṉikkuccil amippatum, atil avaḷi irukka vippatum uṇṭu eṉpatik kuccilukkuḷ irukkiṟāḷ eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkam veḷippaṭuttum. pūppuviḻā muṭintapiṉ kuccili erittal cila malivāṇar vaḻakkeṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.
cōliyaṅ kataṉil umpi pulliṉāl toṭutta tūya
cāliyuḷ iruntāḷ aiya tavamceyta tavamām tiyal-cun. 	tiruvaṭi. 			64
eṉavarum kampar vākku, paraṇacāli yamippi viḷakkum.
kāṭu kāvalarum ēri kāvalarum kaḷaṅkāvalarum kuccil amittalum, māṟi māṟi iravu pakalākak kāval iruttalum innāḷilum kāṇak kūṭiya kāṭcikaḷē!
iṉip paṉṉa cāli eṉpatoru peyarum ataṟkuṇṭu. paṉṉutal, piṉṉutal. ōli ili taḻikaḷik koṇṭu paṉṉutalāl vantateṉka. paṉṉaka cāli eṉpatum atu.
ivaṉ, kikaḷ iṉṟu paṉṉacāli kaṭṭa valla vāyavē eṉpatoru paḻiya irāmāyaṇap pāṭal (puṟat 725).
paraṇam:
paraṇam eṉpataṟkut tāṅkutal eṉpatoru poruḷ. vaṟiyarāy vantāri varavēṟṟu utavum pēruḷamē ivaṇ paraṇamātal aṟika.
aṭukkiya mūvulakum kēṭkumē cāṉṟōr
koṭuttār eṉappaṭuñ col
eṉpatu nālaṭippāṭṭu (100). pukaḻ paravutaṟku iṭaṉāka irukkum koṭiyip paraṇam eṉṟami ataṉ paravutal taṉmi karutiyatēyām. iṉi, mārpakam muḻutuṟat taḻuvik kāvaṟkaṭaṉ ceyyum meymmaṟi (kavacam) paraṇam eṉappaṭum. paraṇi eṉṉum viṇmīṉum paraṇam eṉappaṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa akarātikaḷ. ataṉ poruṭ poruttam paraṇiyiṟ kāṇka!
paraṇar:
kaḻakak kālapperum pulavaruḷ oruvar paraṇar. akanāṉūṟu, kuṟuntoki, naṟṟiṇi, patiṟṟuppattu, puṟanāṉūṟu ākiya toki nūlkaḷil ivar pāṭalkaḷ uṇṭu. kapila paraṇar eṉac cāṉṟōrāl eṭuttāḷappaṭutal ivartam pulamic ciṟappiyum kapilarkkum ivarkkum irunta peruṅ kiḻamiyiyum urikkum.
iṉṟum paraṇaṉ pāṭiṉaṉ maṟkol
eṉavarum (puṟam. 99) auviyār moḻi, paraṇartam ciṟappiṉi naṉku pakarum.
kaṭal piṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ patiṟṟuppattil aintām pattup pāṭiyataṟkup paricilāka umpaṟkāṭṭu varuvāyip paraṇarukku uvantu tantatuṭaṉ, taṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliyum paricilāka vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ eṉa varum ceyti (patiṟ. pati. 5) piṉṉavaṉi maṉṉavaṉ takuti ākkutaṟku vallavar iṉṉavarē eṉat tērntu ceyta cīrttic ceyalām. ivar kālattiṟkup piṉṉarum ivar peyarāl palar iruntu paravu pukaḻāḷarākat tikaḻntami ivar pukaḻi malimēl viḷakkeṉak kāṭṭum. iṉi, ivar paraṇi nāḷiṟ piṟantavarātal vēṇṭum. atu karuti ippeyar peṟṟār eṉpar. āyiṉ, paraṇiyārāka iruppārē yaṉṟip paraṇar eṉappaṭār eṉpatu teḷivāṉa ceyti.
varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷit tam pāṭalkaḷil virittum cuṭṭiyum cellum ip pulavar perumakaṉār, nāṭaḷāviya pukaḻāḷarākat tikaḻntār eṉpatu orutaliyām. acciṟappē ivaric cāṉṟōrkaḷāl paraṇar eṉa vaḻaṅkumāṟu ceytatām eṉalām. nam kaṇkāṇap periyār eṉa oruvar peyarē amintu viṭavilliyā! āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē periyāḻvār eṉa oruvar peyar iruntatilliyā?
paraṇi:
paraṇi eṉpatoru viṇmīṉ; aṭuppu, mukkūṭṭukkal pōṉṟa amippu uṭimiyāl paraṇi aṭuppu eṉavum paṭum. aṭuppu eṉṉum collukkup paraṇi eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pariya cāṭiyum, cilantik kūṭum, ēri nīrp perukki vaḻiya viṭum matakum, kāl kikaḷi akaṟṟic curukki āṭum kūttum ākiyavi paraṇiyiṉ poruḷaṉa. ivi paravutal, akalutal, virital poruṇmi yuṭiyavi eṉpati aṟika. aṭupput taṉittu oṉṟāka vikkum vaḻakkamilli eṉpati aṟiyiṉ, piḷḷi yaṭuppu allatu kuṭṭiyaṭuppu, pakka aṭuppu, koṭi yaṭuppu eṉa aṭuppukaḷ uṇmi viḷaṅkum.
paraṇi aṭuppup pāḻ pōkātu
paraṇiyilē piṟantāl taraṇi yāḷalām
eṉṉum paḻamoḻikaḷum,
taraṇi kāvalaṉ caccantaṉ eṉpavaṉ
paraṇi nāṭpiṟan tāṉpaki yāviyum
araṇi lāṉ
eṉṉum cintāmaṇiyum, iṉṉa nāṭpiṟanta iṉṉāṉ eṉṟal aracarkku marapu. paraṇi yāṉi piṟanta nāḷātaliṉ atu pōlap pakiyi matiyāṉ eṉṟāṉ eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar viḷakkavuriyum, paraṇiyāṉ pāravaṉ eṉṉum mayilinātar uriyum (naṉ. 50) ivaṇ karutik koḷḷattakkaṉa.
paraṇi nūl:
pōriṭi āyiram āṇyāṉikaḷ paṭṭaḻiya, veṉṟa vīramakaṉukkup pāṭappaṭuvatu paraṇi nūlākum. 
āṉi āyiram amariṭi veṉṟa
māṉava ṉukku vakuppatu paraṇi	- ilak. pāṭ. 78
āṉip pōr veṟṟiyāḷarkkaṉṟip piṟarkkup paraṇi vakuppatilli eṉpati,
yāṉi eyta aṭukaḷat talla
tiyāvarum peṟāar paraṇip pāṭṭē	- paṉṉiru. 244
eṉpatāl aṟiyalām.
yāṉit tokiyip piṟavāṟu kūṟiṉārum uḷar.
ēḻtalip peyta nūṟuṭi ipamē
aṭukaḷat taṭṭāṟ pāṭutal kaṭaṉē	- paṉṉiru. 245
eṉpatu paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal.
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, kaṭittiṟappu mutaliya pala uṟuppukaḷi amittup pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉuṭiya palavakic ciṟappukaḷiyum palvēṟu vakikaḷāl puṟapporuḷ amiti tōṉṟa viḷakkik kalittāḻiciyāl paraṇi pāṭap peṟum. itaṉuḷ iru cīraṭi, muccīraṭi yuṭiyavi vārā.
paraṇiyiṉ poruṭṭoṭar nili varumāṟu:
tēvar vāḻttē kaṭinili pāli
mēvi amarum kāḷi kōyil
kaṉṉiyi ēttal alaki vinōtam
kaṉānili nimittam paciyē ōki
peruntēvi pīṭam aḻakuṟa irukka
amarnili nimittam avaḷpatam paḻiccā
maṉṉavaṉ vāki maliyum aḷavum
marapiṉi turittal maṟakkaḷaṅ kāṇṭal
cerumiku kaḷattiṭi aṭukūḻ vārttal
paravutal iṉṉa varuvaṉa piṟavum
toṭarnili yākac collutal kaṭaṉē	- paṉṉiru. 243
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kaṭitiṟap purittal
kaṭumpāli kūṟal koṭuṅkāḷi kōṭṭam
kaṭikaṇam urittal kāḷik kitucolal
aṭupēyk kavaḷcolal ataṉāl talivaṉ
vaṇpukaḻ urittal eṇpuṟat tiṇiyuṟa
vīṭṭal aṭukaḷam vēṭṭal ivimēl
aḷavaṭi mutalā aṭiyiraṇ ṭāka
uḷamakiḻ paraṇi urikkap paṭumē	- ilak. pāṭ. 79
paraṇippōr muṟiyip paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal,
mayakkaṟu koccakat tīraṭi iyaṉṟu
nayappuṟu tāḻici uṟuppiṟ potintu
vañci malinta uḻiñi muṟṟit
tumpiyiṟ ceṉṟa toṭukaḻal maṉṉaṉi
vempuciṉa māṟṟāṉ tāṉiveṅ kaḷattiṟ
kurutip pērāṟu perukuñ ceṅkaḷat
torutaṉi ēttum paraṇiyatu paṇpē	- paṉṉiru. 242
eṉak kūṟum. navanītap pāṭṭiyalō paraṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇatti aintu kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟikaḷāl viriyak kūṟum (57-61).
paraṇi eṉpatu nāṇmīṉiṉ peyar. paraṇi nāḷ mutaṉmutal aṭuppil tīmūṭṭi aṭutaṟku uṟṟa nāḷ eṉpar. an nāḷit tērntu aṭukaḷattuṟṟa pēykaḷ kūḻaṭṭu uṇṭu kūttāṭi makiḻvatāka aminta nūlukkup peyarāyiṟṟu. paraṇi nāṭ peyarpporuḷ,
kāṭu kiḻavōḷ pūtam aṭuppē
tāḻi peruñcōṟu tarumaṉāḷ pōtameṉap
pāku paṭṭatu paraṇināṭ peyarē
eṉṟu tivākaram kūṟutalāl pulappaṭum. ip poruḷkaḷukkum paraṇiyil varum ceytikaḷukkum uḷḷa neruṅkiya toṭarpu paraṇi nūlāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
parattami:
kaṭṭiṉṟip palariṉpam tēṭi vāḻum vāḻvip parattami vāḻveṉpatu paḻami toṭṭē varum vaḻakku.
naṇṇēṉ parattaniṉ mārpu
eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (1311).
parattantu eṉpatoru col paripāṭalil varukiṉṟatu (7.39) ataṟkup parimēlaḻakar parakka eṉa uri eḻutiṉār. parattara eṉṉum collik kaliyum (106), puṟamum (274) vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. parattalic ceyyumpaṭi eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām (kali. nac.).
parattal, parattarutal pōla vantavi parattaṉ, paratti, parattar, parattami eṉṉum coṟkaḷām. nakarap parattar eṉap parattari iṭañcuṭṭi yurikkiṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷār (cilap. 5:200). parattar, kaḻikāmukar eṉṟār itaṟku uriviḷakkam tarum aṭiyārkku nallār. iṉip parattar iyaṉmiyi viḷakkuvār pōla, vampap parattar eṉṟār aṭikaḷār (16:23). itaṟku viḷakkam variyum aṭiyārkku nallār, vampap parattar= putiya kāma nukarcciyi virumpum kāmukar; parattiyi nukarvāṉum parattaṉ eṉṟār. ittaṉmi paṇpil kātalaṉ parattami eṉa maṇimēkaliyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟatu (7:50).
pōṉa pōkkilē parantupōm veḷḷattiyum, kōlak kuṟuṅkaṇum (cāḷaram) nuḻiyum teṉṟaliyum muṟiyē paripāṭalum cilampum uvami kāṭṭuvatu koṇṭē, uṇmi tāṉē viḷaṅkum. parattaṉ mutalāya iccoṟkaḷ paravutal, appāl, appālukku appāl eṉa varituṟiyiṉṟi akalutal eṉṉum poruṭpaṭa amintiruttal aṟiyat takkaṉavē.
aṟivarāyiṉum tavattarāyiṉum putitu putituṇṇum vaṇṭu pōlum kāmukarāyiṉum kaṇvali yāl paṟṟip piṭikkum kalivalla - eṇṇeṇ kalivalla - parattiyaric cuṭṭukiṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. avarkaḷit taḷiyilār eṉṟum patiyilār eṉṟum arumpata vuriyāciriyarum aṭiyārkku nallārum urikkiṉṟaṉar (cilap. 14:160, 167). parākan tēṭiyuṇṇum vaṇṭukku uvami kūṟiya oṉṟāl kaṇṭu koḷḷalāmē.
parattaṉ:
param + attu + aṉ = parattaṉ.
puṟattoḻukkamuṭiyavaṉ parattaṉām. parattaṉ eṉpatu illiṉ kaṭṭut tāṇṭiya kaṭṭaṟṟa vāḻviṉaṉ eṉpatām. paratti eṉpatu payila vaḻaṅkiṉum pālvēṟupāṭaṟṟup parattaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiyamiyum aṟiyalām.
māyap parattaṉ vāymoḻi nampi	- akam. 146
naṇṇēṉ parattaniṉ mārpu	- tiruk. 1311
parattiyiṟ pirivu:
pālit tiṇi eṉpatu pirital oḻukkam paṟṟiyatu. appirivu vakikaḷi, 
ōtal pakiyē tūtivi pirivē	- akam. 25
poruḷvayiṉ piritalum avarvayiṉ urittē	- akat 33
kāvaṟ pāṅku parattiyiṉ akaṟci (akat. 41) eṉap palatiṟappaṭa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār vakuttārākap piṉṉuḷōr, parattiyiṟ piriveṉa oru pirivākkiyatuṭaṉ, ilvāḻkkiyē parattiyiṟ pirivē eṉa mutaṉmi taravum āyiṉar. cērip paratti, iṟparatti eṉap piṟapiṟa peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkum, paḻanāḷ toṭṭē āyiṟṟu. ataṉi mutaṟkaṇ kaṭintu koṭi tūkki muḻakkamiṭṭavar mutaṟpāvalarē eṉpatu ivaṇ eṇṇattakkatu.
paratti:
paratti eṉpatoru col, parattami cuṭṭāp poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu paravi eṉappaṭum. kaṭalāḷukiyuṭiya paratavar kuṭippeṇ makaḷik kuṟippatu. paratavar paravar ākiya piṉ avartam peṇṭir paratti eṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉka! paravar, paṭavar eṉavum cempaṭavar eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal ivaṇ aṟika!


paratti:
iṉip paratti eṉṟoru col parattami kuṟiyāp poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu colliṉ muḻuvaṭivaṟṟa col. cemparatti eṉpataṉ kuṟiccol. oru ceṭivaki atu.
parantōr:
parantōr ellām pukaḻ eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil varum parantōr paravi niṉṟōr eṉṉum poruḷatām (285). parantōr aṟivāṉ virintōr eṉṟum pukaḻāl virintōr eṉṟum poruḷtara āṭciyil uṇṭu. iruṅkuṉ ṟeṉṉum peyar paran tatuvē eṉṉum paripāṭal (15:35).
peyar parantatu eṉa viḷakkik kāṭṭutal aṟika. parantu paṭu nallici eyti eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟum, parantupaṭṭa nalla pukaḻi ivvulakattup porunti eṉṉum ataṉ uriyum ivaṇ karutattakkaṉa (213).
parappu:
parappu:1
ulaku parappāl periyatu eṉpatu ulakaṟinta ceyti. ataṉāl parappakam, pārakam, akal ñālam, kaṇṇakal ñālam, māñālam eṉa eṇṇaṟṟa vakiyāl kuṟikkappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
ivvulakiṉ nilapparappiṉum nīrpparappē mummaṭaṅku virintatu eṉpatum ulakaṟinta ceyti. ataṉi uṇarttumāp pōla nīrp parappukkup paravi eṉṟoru peyar cūṭṭiṉar nam muntiyōr. parappu eṉpatu nilapparappu nīrpparappu eṉap potumiyuṭiyatāyiṉum parappu kaṭalic ciṟappākak kuṟippatum āyiṟṟu.
parantakaṉṟa nīrppakutiyip paravi eṉṟatum parappu eṉṟatum tērcci mikkatām. orē pārviyil pārkka muṭiyāta parappu mikkatu nīr eṉpati iccoṟkaḷ collum poruḷum oruṅkē kuṟippatu pōl maṟṟoru col kuṟippākak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. atu kaṭal eṉpatu.
kaṭpārviyik kaṭantu viriyum virivuṭiya nīrpparappu kaṭal eṉka! ikkaṭaliṉ virivup poruḷ ilakkaṇak kaṭal ilakkiyak kaṭal aṟivuk kaṭal kaṭalaṉṉa celvam naṉmi kaṭaliṟ peritu (tiruk. 103) eṉṟellām iruvaki vaḻakku kaḷilum ūṉṟalāyiṉa.
paravi eṉpatu neytal nilam. annilattiṟkuriya makkaḷ paratavar, paravar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṭaṟpakutiyi āṭci ceyta vēntarkaḷ paratava eṉṟu viḷikkap peṟṟaṉar.
kaṭalo ṭuḻanta paṉittuṟip paratava
eṉpatu patiṟṟuppattu (48).
ivar paratar eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatu paṭar tirip paratar eṉavarum kampar vākkālum (kārkālap. 74),
parata kumararum
eṉa varum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ vākkālum (cilap. 5:168) aṟiyappeṟum.
paratavar vēntarum, paratavar viṉiñarumāka iru tiṟattavarum iruntaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
teṉparatavar pōrēṟē eṉavarum maturikkāñci (144) maṉṉarik kuṟikkum; mīṉvilip paratavar eṉavarum cilampu (5:25) nilamakkaḷām viṉimakkaḷik kuṟikkum. koṭuntimil paratavar kurūuc cuṭar eṇṇutalip paṭṭiṉappāliyum (112) paḻampaṭu tēṟal paratavar parukutalic ciṟupāṇum (159) pāṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aracarkaḷāl eṭṭip paṭṭam peṟṟavarākavum, kaṭal vaṇikarākavum iruntamiyāl vaṇikarum piṉṉāḷil paratavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. paratavar kōttiratta mintaṉ (upatēca. civat 189) eṉpati aṟika.
paratavar eṉpār paratar eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟa kuṟippum palanūlkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aṟiyap peṟukiṉṟatu. paratar malinta palvēṟu teruvik kāṭṭukiṟatu perumpāṇ (323). paratar tanta palvēṟu kūlattik kāṭṭukiṟatu maturikkāñci (317).
parappu:
parappu:2
paravac ceytal, akala virittal ākiya poruḷ vaḻiyākap parapputal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar varum. parappu eṉa ēvalākavum peyarākavum niṟṟal uṇṭu. parappu iṭavirivu eṉṉum poruḷatu. maṇṇiṭam, vāṉiṭam, kaṭaliṭam ākiya iṭa virivukaḷip potuvākac cuṭṭalum, taṉittaṉiyē ciṟappākac cuṭṭalum uṇṭu.
neṟparappu, nilipparappu eṉṟiru vakiyāṉa nila aḷavic cuṭṭukiṉṟatu ce.pa.ka. akarāti. katavu niliyiṉ mēluḷa maṇtāṅkip palakiyiyum parappu eṉaluṇṭu.
kaṇakku vakiyil parappaḷavu kāṇal eṉpatoṉṟu. parappaḷavukkeṉat tuṟiyē amintuḷḷatu. virivu, cuṟucuṟuppu, tiṉaveṭuttal eṉṉum poruḷil paraparattal paraparappu eṉpavi vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa.
oḻuṅkāka iru; illiyāṉāl aṭi vāṅkuvāy eṉṉum poruḷil, uṭampu eṉṉa paraparakkiṟatā? eṉṟu viṉāvutal kēṭkak kūṭiya vaḻakku. parappāḻ, papparappāḻ eṉpaṉa tiṟantaveḷi, vāṉveḷi eṉpavaṟṟip potumakkaḷ iyalpākak kuṟikkum vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ. iviyellām paravutal virital poruḷaṉa eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
param:
mēlāṉatu, uyarntatu, appālāṉatu, appaḻukku illātatu mutaliya poruḷkaḷit tarum col param eṉpatām. paramporuḷ, paramaṉ, paratēci, paramāṉatu eṉpavaṟṟil ip poruḷkaḷ muṟiyē amintiruttal aṟika.
kaṭavuḷ eṉpatiluḷḷa kaṭanta eṉṉum poruḷ param eṉpatilum uṇmiyik kaṭal paravi eṉpavaṟṟoṭum eṇṇik koḷka!
moḻipeyar tēyattarē irantuṇpārāka irunta nāḷil eḻunta collāṭci paratēyi eṉpatām. paratēci eṉa vaḻaṅkutal tēem tēm tēyam eṉpavi tēcam eṉa vēṟṟuc collākak koḷḷappaṭṭataṉ piṉṉi vaṭivamām.
param eṉpatu pāram eṉṉum poruḷilum varum. paraviya iṭam pār eṉappaṭutal pōl, paraviya cumi pāram āyiṟṟu. pāravaṇṭi, pāraccaṭṭam, pār eṉpavaṟṟi eṇṇuka!
niripparap poṟiya narippuṟak kaḻuti eṉak kaḻutiyiṉ mutukil irupālum cumi oḻuṅkupaṭa amittuc cellum muṟimi akappāṭṭilē viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu (207). kaḻutiyiṉ mutukup parappiṉum mikku irupālum nirantu parantu kiṭakkum cumi niripparam eṉṉum collāṭciyāl viḷaṅkutal nayamikkatām. mēl mēl aṭukkalum niripparamām.
kaḷḷamillāta veḷḷit taṉmiyip param eṉpatum, attakiyarip paramāṉavar eṉpatum, avartam pōkkiṉic - ceyaliṉip - parampōkku eṉpatum vaḻakkil uḷḷami aṟiyattakkaṉa. paramēḷam eṉpatum parampōkkē.
akaṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya akalvāyc caṭṭiyip paravac caṭṭi eṉpatum. ayalē iruntu varum vākkip paravākku eṉpatum niṉiyalām.
uṭalukkup param eṉpatoru colluṇṭu. param, pāram eṉṉum poruḷatu. uṭalik koṇṭē uyirvāḻvu iyalvatu eṉṟālum, uṭalik koṇṭē aruḷ toṇṭu iyalvatu eṉṟālum, avvuṭalip pēṇikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tīrāk kaṭaṉum iruttalāl, avarkkut tammuṭampu pēṇutalum cumiyāy amikiṉṟatām. ataṉālaṉṟē, 
maṟṟum toṭarpā ṭevaṉkol piṟappaṟukkal
uṟṟārk kuṭampum miki
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (tiruk. 345). ipparantuṭittavar eṉṉum kampar vākku (kaṭimaṇap. 69) param eṉpataṟku uṭal eṉṉum poruḷuṇmi kāṭṭum.
paramparam:
paramparam appālukku appālām vīṭu pēṟṟik kuṟippatu uṇṭu. patticey aṭiyārip param parattu uyppavaṉ eṉṉum tiruvācakam (2. 119) itaṉit teḷivikkum. paramparattu uyppavaṉ paramparaṉ (tiruvāy. 4.3:9) eṉka!
oṉṟaṟku mēl oṉṟāy uyarntu viḷaṅki varutal parampari eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ceṉṟu ceṉṟu paramparamāy eṉavarum tiruvāymoḻi (8.8:5) itaṉik kāṭṭum.
parampu:
akalamātal parampu. parapputal vaḻiyāka eṇṇiṉ parampu teḷivākum. parampaṭittal uḻavut toḻilil oru pakuti. ataṟkeṉap parampuc caṭṭam uṇṭu. iṅkē parampu eṉṉum col pāy eṉṉum poruḷil viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. pāy eṉpatum paramputal poruḷatē yākum.
kallāñcaraḷi paravikkiṭakkum vaṟaṇṭa nilam parampu eṉappaṭum. paraviya nilam eṉṉum poruḷil parampu āḷappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. parampelām pavaḷam eṉṉum kampar vākku (nāṭṭup. 2) nilapparappi atilum maruta nilapparappik kāṭṭuvatē. varampu varappeṉa valittal aṭivatu pōlap parampu parappeṉa ākum. parampu aḷavu eṉpatē parappaḷavu āyatu veḷippaṭi. parappu eṉpatu ceṇṭu veḷi eṉap poruḷ tarutal peruṅkatiyil kaṇṭatu (3.5:10). ceṇṭu veḷiyāvatu kutiriyōṭum veḷi, muṟṟam eṉpaṉa.
parampaṭittal:
uḻavut toḻilil, parampaṭittal eṉpatoṉṟu. atu toḻi uḻutu, kuḻi mititta piṉ parampuc caṭṭattāl camaṉilip paṭuttum paṇiyām. ataṟkuriya caṭṭam parampuc caṭṭam eṉappaṭum. iṉip palakiyiyē parampākap parappi aṭippatum allatu iḻuppatum vaḻakkē! atu parampup palaki eṉappaṭum.
kulappoṉṉit tirunāṭar parampaṭikka
eṉpatu ēr eḻupatu (26).
parar:
parar eṉpār ayalār; appālār, pararkkaṭimi ceytiṭōm eṉpatil (pāratiyār pāṭal) ayalār maṭṭumaṉṟip pakivar eṉṉum poruḷum uḷatātal aṟika. parar eṉpataṟkup pakivar eṉṉum poruḷuḷatātalic cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu collum.
pararoṭu kēḷār ollār pakivarpēr eḻumūṉ ṟāmē
eṉpatu atu (2:51).
paral:
paravik kiṭakkum murampu nilattu allatu mēṭṭu nilattuk kal paral eṉappaṭum. cukkāṉkal paral eṉpatum, vittu paral eṉpatum, cilampu, kaḻal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷiṭu kal paral eṉpatum ataṉ vaṭivoppuk karutiyatām.
paraṟkal eṉpatu parukkikkal eṉavum āḷappaṭum. paral eṉpatu nilattik kuṟittu vanta peyarāyum. parukki eṉpatu kallik kuṟittu vanta peyarāyum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
murampu kaṇṇuṭinta paralavaṟ pōḻvil
eṉa malipaṭukaṭāmum (198),
veyiluruppuṟṟa vemparal
eṉac ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭiyum (8) paralik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
cilampil maṇiyum muttum paralāka iṭappaṭum eṉpatic cilappatikāram kāṭṭum. paravic ceṉṟu oliyuṇṭākkum vaṇṇam iṭiveḷi paṭak kiṭattalāl avi paral eṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉak koḷḷak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉavām.
paravak kāli:
āṭi ōṭit tiriyum āṭumāṭukaḷip paravakkāli eṉpatu nāṭṭu vaḻakku. avvāṟu alintu tirivāṉum paravakkāli eṉappaṭuvāṉ. avvāṟu alintu tiriyum taṉmiyum paravak kālittaṉam eṉac collappaṭum. ivi potumakkaḷ vaḻakkil uḷḷavi.
paravayam:
taṉṉip paṟṟiya kavaliyillāmal taṉṉic cārntataṉ allatu cārntavar vaṇṇamāki makiḻvatu paravayam eṉappaṭum. param + vayam = paravayam; paravayamāvatu makiḻcci taṉvaya miḻakkiyām. ataṉi,
mayalelām oḻintu paravacamām kālam
eṉpār civappirakācar.
ilivayamākat tanta koṭipporuḷ veṟṟiliyil vittut tanta poruḷ - ilavacam eṉṟāṉāṟ pōlap paravayam paravacam āyiṟṟām.
paravar eṉpār paratavar eṉpatik kaṇṭuḷḷōm. paravutal, paraval eṉpavi paravic cellutal vaḻivanta coṟkaḷē. paravalākap pēcappaṭutal eṉpatu palarālum palaviṭattum pēcap paṭutalām. paravutal vāḻttutal poruḷil varum eṉpati muṉṉarē kaṇṭuḷḷōm. parāvutal eṉpatum apporuḷatē,
ēṉi oṉṟē,
tēvarp parāaya muṉṉilik kaṇṇē
eṉat tēvarp parāvum kalivakiyik kuṟippār tolkāppiyar (cey. 137).
paravukkaṭaṉ:
teyvattiṟku nērntu koṇṭa nērvu allatu nērttik kaṭaṉ paravukkaṭaṉ eṉappaṭum. koṟṟavikkup paravukkaṭaṉ pūṇalik kuṟikkiṟatu tolkāppiyam (poruḷ. 58).
paraveḷi:
param + veḷi = paraveḷi.
paraveḷi iṟiyuṟiyum vāṉiṭam eṉappaṭum. viṇveḷi eṉpatum atu. paraveḷiyi vaṟitu niliiya kāyam (30) eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu kūṟum. ōr ātāramum iṉṟit tāṉē niṟkiṉṟa ākāyam eṉpatu itaṉ paḻiyavuri. kāyam eṉṉum teṉcol ākāra muṉṉoṭṭuk koṇṭu ākāyamāy vaṭacollāyiṟṟu.
paravi:1
parantu kiṭakkum kaṭalukkup paravi eṉpatu paḻampeyar. pārtta pārvikkuḷ aṭaṅkāmal paravik kiṭappatāl peṟṟa peyar. paraviyām kaṭaluḷ mīṉvaḷam koḷḷavum, muttuk kuḷikkavum, kaṭalkaṭantu vaṇikam ceyyavum ākiya tolpaḻan tamiḻar paratavar eṉappaṭṭār. paratavar malinta payaṅkeḻu mānakar eṉak kaṭal nakarkaḷ - paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ - pārāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. nāvalan tēyam paratavar nāṭu eṉa iruntu, piṉṉē āriyak katippuṉivāl parataṉ (cakuntaliyiṉ makaṉ) peyarāl āyatu eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṭalāṇṭa vēntarkaḷ paratavar eṉappaṭṭatum, paratava eṉa viḷikkappaṭṭatum paḻañceyti. innāḷil paratavar kuṭi, mīṉavar paravar eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. paḻaṅkālak kaṭalōṭikaḷ ulakappukaḻ peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉar. muciṟi, toṇṭi, koṟki, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam iṉṉaviyellām tuṟimukaṅkaḷ, vaḷanakarkaḷ.
paravi:2
kaṭal eṉṉum poruḷtarum paraviyi muṉṉark kaṇṭuḷḷōm.
paḻam puṇṇuṟṟāṉ oruvaṉ paṭukkiyil puraḷkiṟāṉ; avaṉ puraḷal pōl ali puraḷkiṟatu kaṭalil; atu, muḻaveṉa muḻaṅkavum ceykiṉṟatu (naṟ. 394).
muḻaṅkutiri muḻaviṉ pāṇiyiṉ pipayap
paḻampuṇ ṇuṟunariṉ paraviyiṉ ālum
eṉpatu atu. ivaṇ paravi puraḷaṟ poruḷatu; puraḷal paravaltāṉē!
paravi yamutu eṉpatu uppiyum, paraviyāḻ eṉpatu pēriyāḻiyum paravi vaḻakku eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkiyum kuṟikkum. paravip pulvari eṉpatu, paranta pulveḷiyil kālnaṭi mēytaṟkākap peṟappaṭṭa oru varippaṇamākum.
paraviyār:
āṭal aḻaku kalinalam ākiyavaṟṟāl paraviya pukaḻ vāynta oruvar paravi nācciyār; cuntarar maṉiyāṭṭiyaruḷ oruvar. avar peyarik kuṟikkum cēkkiḻār,
pērparavi peṇmiyiṉiṟ perumparavi virumpalkul
ārparavi yaṇitikaḻum aṇimuṟuval arumparavi
cīrparavi yāyiṉāḷ tiruvuruviṉ meṉcāyal
ērparavi yiṭippaṭṭa eṉṉāci eḻuparavi	- periya. taṭut 148
eṉkiṟār.
paraṉ:
par + aṉ = paraṉ.
ayaṉmiyāṉ eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatuṭaṉ, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ tarum col paraṉ. poṟipulaṉkaḷukku appālāṉ eṉṉum iṟimik koḷki vaḻi vanta col paraṉ; iṉip param + aṉ = paramaṉ eṉṟum, īroṭṭukaḷ cērntum iṟivaṉik kuṟikkum, paramāya poruḷ paramporuḷ eṉa muntu kaṇṭatiyum niṉika! paraṉ civaṉ, tirumāl arukaṉ mutalām pala kaṭavuḷariyum kuṟikkum collākat tamiḻ nikaṇṭukaḷum avvac camaya nūlkaḷum cuṭṭutal aṟiyattakkatu.
par - parā:
para eṉpatu parā eṉṟākiyum paravutal poruḷ tarum.
parā:
parakkap pārttal parākkup pārttalāka varum eṉpatik kuṟittuḷḷōm. parākam eṉpatu pūmparākam eṉappaṭum pūmpoṭi (makarantappoṭi) ataṉ poruḷiṉṟu paravum tūḷ, poṭi ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟippatākavum parākam amintatu. parām eṉat tokuttum apporuḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu.
paravik kiṭakkum pālivaṉam parāṭam eṉavum, parutta aṭimaram parāri eṉavum, parutta mēltoṭi parāri eṉavum (poruna. 104) vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. paravutal parāvutalāka varutal aṟintatē!
parākku:
* kaṭṭiyam kāṇka.
parāḷam:
par + āḷam = parāḷam.
parāḷam eṉpatu paraparappu (paṭapaṭappu) eṉṉum poruḷil nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. par akalamātal poruḷatu. eṇṇam, kāl, ki oru kaṭṭukkuḷ nillāmal akalappaṭutal vaḻi ēṟpaṭṭa collākum. ērāḷam, tārāḷam eṉpavi pōl āḷam collīṟu. āḷappaṭutal āḷam.
par - pari:
par eṉṉum vēruṭaṉ ikaram cērtalāl pari eṉṉum colvaṭivam peṟukiṉṟatu. akaram cērtalāl par eṉpatu paravutal poruḷ tantatu pōl, ikaram cērtalāl vaḷital poruḷ tarukiṉṟatām.
paraviya oṉṟu vaḷital iyaṟki; ulakiyal attakitteṉa uṟṟu nōkkuvār teḷivāka aṟivar.
ulakukku aṇṭam eṉpatoru peyar; ataṉ vaṭivam vaṭṭam eṉpatik kāṭṭum peyar atu. viti, muṭṭi eṉpaṉavum aṇṭam eṉpataṉ poruḷavē. apporuḷ vaṭivam vaṭṭattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭaṉavē.
tiṅkaḷ ñāyiṟu viṇmīṉ ivaṟṟiṉ vaṭivellām vaṭṭamē. kaṭal valayam eṉpatāl kaṭal vaṭṭam puriyum. nīrārum kaṭaluṭutta nilam, puṭavikku aṇitukil eṉavaḷar antak kaṭal eṉpaṉa kaṭal vaṭṭam kuṟippaṉa.
nīrniliyil orukal viḻuntāl vaṭṭam kiḷarntu vaḷarntu vaḷarntu peruvaṭṭamātal aṟiyār evar? nīrccuḻi vaṭṭamē! maḻittuḷi vaṭivam vaṭṭamē. atu viḻum vaṭivam vaṭṭamē. kāṟṟuk kiḷarvatum tī eḻunterivatum vaṭṭamē. vāṉiṉ tōṟṟam vaṭṭamē. āṭu māṭu mutalām vilaṅkukaḷ paṭuttal vaṭṭamē. āṭiyiṉṟi vāṭiyil meliyum māntaṉ paṭuttirukkum niliyum vaṭṭamē. paṟavik kūṭukaḷ vaṭṭamē. avaṟṟiṉ ulāvalum, vaṇṭiṉ cuḻalvum vaṭṭamē. pūvum vittum kaṉiyum kāyum tavacamum vaṭṭamē. taṉittaṉi colvāṉēṉ? 
kuḻanti kiyil kariyō cuṇṇamō eḻutukōlō kiṭittāl iyalpāka varivatu vaṭṭamē. vaḷiyāmal vaṭṭamētu?
ēṉ? kōṭu eṉpatē vaḷivu eṉṉum poruḷatē. kōṭṭam, kōṭṭakam, kōṭṭi, kōṭṭi iviyum vaḷivē! kaṇi koṭitu yāḻkōṭu cevvitu eṉṟārē vaḷḷuvar. kōṭu vaḷivu aṉṟō? malikkuk kōṭum, marattiṟkuk kōṭum vantami vaḷivāltāṉē!
ākaliṉ paravum oṉṟu vaḷital allatu vaṭṭamātal iyaṟki niyati. itu koṇṭu par eṉpatoṭu ikaram cērnta pari vaḻic coṟkaḷ vaḷital poruḷ tarutal tamiḻiyal eṉka!
vaḷitalāl vanta peyar vaḷi, vaḷiyal, vaḷivu, vaḷiyam, vaḷacal, vaḷākam ivaṟṟil vaḷivu, ōraḷavum uṇṭu; pēraḷavum uṇṭu. muḻutuṟum vaḷivum uṇṭu. pari mutaṟcoṟkaḷ vaḷitaṟ poruḷil varutalik kāṇpōm. vaḷitaliṉ aṭik karuttu eppaṭi eppaṭi amiyumeṉa viḷakkukiṟār pāvāṇar.
vaḷitaṟ karuttu iyaṉ muṟiyil vaḷivu, curuṭci, vaṭṭam, vaḷiyam, uruṇṭi, uruḷi mutaliya paṇpuk karuttuk kaḷiyum, ceyaṉmuṟiyil vaḷital, curuḷtal, tirital, cūḻtal, cuṟṟutal, uruḷutal mutaliya viṉik karuttukkaḷiyum taḻuvum.
vaḷivu eṉṉum paṇpiṉ peyarum, vaḷital eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ peyarum appaṇpiyum viṉiyiyum koṇṭa poruḷkaḷi ākupeyarākak kuṟikkum.
kōṇal, cāytal eṉpaṉa vaḷitaliṉ muntiya nilikaḷātaliṉ aviyum ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum. (vērccoṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ, 155).
pari:1
accattālō, oru nōkkattālō ōṭum ōṭṭam aṉṟi maṟṟi ōṭṭam ellā uyirkaḷukkum potuvāka vaṭṭamiṭal, vaḷitalākavē iyalum. avvakiyil taṉ peyarip peṟṟa vilaṅku kutiri. atu kutittuk kutittu ōṭutalāl kutirip peyarum vaḷiyamiṭṭuc cellutalāl parip peyarum peṟṟatu. ataṉ āṟṟal tiṟamē, aṟiviyal mūḷiyiṟ pariyāṟṟalāy (Horse Power) miṉṉaḷavīṭāka iyalkiṉṟatu.
vaḷitalāl parippeyar peṟṟataṉ naṭiyum ōṭṭamum pari eṉappaṭṭaṉa. kālē pari tappiṉa eṉṉum kuṟuntoki celaviyum (14), parīi eṉpatu kutirik katiyām ōṭṭattiyum (puṟam. 4) kuṟittaṉa. kutiri ōṭṭattiṟkuriya viyāḷi vīti puravivaṭṭam eṉappaṭutal aṟiyat takkatu (cūṭā. 5:46). kutiri vaṭiva viṇmīṉām acuvati pari eṉappaṭuvatiyum cūṭāmaṇi cuṭṭum. ataṟkuc cakaṭam eṉa oru peyar uṇmiyum aṟiyattakkatu. cakaṭam = cakkaram.
kutiri ēṟṟam pariyēṟṟam eṉappaṭutal evarum aṟintatu. kuḷam, ēri, āṟu mutaliyavi nīrpperukkāl uṭippeṭuttu viṭṭāl ataṉi aṭikkap pukuvār valiya marattūṇkaḷi uṭippup pakutiyil niṟutti ataṉ cārpil marakkiḷi maṇalmūṭi mutaliyavaṟṟic ceṟittu uṭippi aṭippatu vaḻakkam. uṭippil niṟuttum marattiṟkup pari eṉpatu peyar. kutiri maram eṉak kūṟuvar. pari niṟuttuvār eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭal, piṭṭukku maṇ cumanta paṭalattu (5) varum ceyti.
puṟappāṭalkaḷil kutiri maṟam taṉippukaḻ vāyntatu. pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ perumporuḷ, kutiri vāṅkutaṟkuc ceṉṟati veḷināṭṭu ulāvāṇar viyanturittuḷar.
pari eṉpatu pariviṉ mūlam, parivāvatu aṉpu. avvaṉpum avvaṉpiṉāl uṇṭām pātukāppum pari eṉappaṭum. pātukāppu eṉpatu piṟar pāram (cumi) tāṅkum pāṉmi ātalāl, ac cumiyum, accumi taṉ cumiyoṭum ēṟum cumiyākaliṉ mikiyum ammiki palar pārāṭṭum paṇpiyal ātalāl uyarvum perumiyum ākiyavellām poruḷākap pari ciṟantaṉavām.
pari vaḷitalātalum aṉpātalum piṟavātalum eppaṭi? āvum kaṉṟum, tāyum cēyum, paṟaviyum kuñcum, cēvalum peṭiyum vaḷiya varutali aṟiyōmō? aṉpiṉāl maṭṭumō vaḷital? vaṉpiṉāl oru kuṭṭikkō kuñcukkō iṭar cūḻa ōruyiri muyaluṅkāl attāy vaḷiya vaḷiya vantu taṉ valuvellām kūṭṭit tākkutali evarē aṟiyār? itaṉāl aṉṟē vaḷḷuvap pērāciriyar,
aṟattiṟkē aṉpucār peṉpa aṟiyār
maṟattiṟkum aḵtē tuṇi
eṉṟār (76). ivaṇ cārac cārac cārntu, tīrat tīrat tīrā aṉpu vaṭṭamātalic cārpuc collāl kuṟittami koḷḷaṟ pālatām. nammi maṟantāri nām maṟakka māṭṭēmāl eṉṉum kātalaṉpiṟ kuṉṟiyatō at tāyaṉpu? kātal muruki muruki vaḷarntu, aruṇilik kātalāyp perukiya tallavō tāymi?
pari eṉpataṟku varuntutal poruḷum uṇṭu. itaṉ mikiyik kāṭṭap paripulamputal eṉṉum āṭciyum uṇṭu.
pakkam cērntu, paripulam piṉaṉivaṉ
tāṉē tamiyaṉ vantaṉaṉ aḷiyaṉ
eṉpatu cāttaṉār vākku (maṇimē. 16:57-58).
aṉpup poruḷām pari varuntutal varuttam āmō! atu varuntākkāl etu varuntuvatu? aruḷāḷar varalāṟukaḷ, tamakku varuntuvatiyō poruṭṭākki yurikkiṉṟaṉa? piṟapiṟa uyirkku iraṅki parintu iraṅki varuntum varuttatti yaṉṟō pukalkiṉṟaṉa. āṭu tūkkic cumanta puttar varalāṟum, tantiyē ivarkaḷip poṟuttaruḷum eṉṟa kiṟittu perumāṉ varalāṟum, vāṭiya payirik kaṇṭa pōtellām vāṭiya vaḷḷalār varalāṟum avarkaḷ piṟa uyirkaḷukkākat tuṭitta tuṭippi yallavō kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkkeṉa vanta allal illāmiyāl aṉṟō, allal aruḷ āḷvārkku illi eṉat tiṭṭamāka urittār tiruvaḷḷuvar. itaṟku vaḷivaḻaṅku mallalmā ñālam kariyāka (cāṉṟāka) irut talik kaṇṭu teḷika eṉa vaḻikāṭṭiṉārē (tiruk. 245). ivaṟṟi ellām eṇṇiṉāl pari eṉpataṟku varuttap poruḷ vanta vaki viḷaṅkum.
pari eṉṉum collukkuk kaṇṭa kutiri eṉṉum poruḷ vaḻiyākac cila coṟkaḷ vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. avi parikkārar, parikkōl, parimā, parimukam eṉpaṉa.
parikkārar āvār kutirikkārar. kutiri naṭattuvōr.
parikkōl eṉpatu kuttukkōl; parikkōṟkārar eṉpār kuttukkōṟkārar; ivar yāṉippākar. matattāṟ, parikkōl elliyil nillāta kaḷiṟṟik kāṭṭukiṟatu tolkāppiya uri (tol. poruḷ. 12).
parimā eṉpatu pariyākiya vilaṅku. karimā, arimā, narimā pōla vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku. parimukam kutirimuka vaṭivāka aminta viṇmīṉ (acuviṇi) parimuka vaṭivil aminta paṭaku parimuka ampi eṉappaṭṭatu. parimuka ampiyum karimuka ampiyum eṉpatu cilampu (13:176) parimuka mākkaḷ eṉpār kiṉṉarar ātalik kampar cuṭṭuvār (cittirakūṭap paṭalam, 11).
pariccāttu eṉpatoru col. cāttu eṉpatu vaṇikak kūṭṭam. vaṇikar talivaṉāka iruntavaṉ mācāttaṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ; avaṉ kōvalaṉ tanti. kutiri vaṇikam pariccāttu eṉappaṭṭu, avvaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭa kuḻuvum ākupeyarāl pariccāttu eṉappaṭṭatu.
vantatu mutuparic cāttu
eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam (28:29).
pari:2
iṟivic cāl caturamāṉatu. pari eṉpatu iṟivik kūṉi eṉappaṭum vaṭṭa vaṭiviṉatu. pari eṉpatu vaṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷil varum mutaṉili. parivaṭṭam, parivēṭam eṉpavi aṟika. pari eṉpatu iṟivik kūṉip poruḷil nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. kūṉ, kūṉal, kūṉi eṉpavi vaḷivē.
parikam pariki:
parikam eṉṉum collukku akaḻ, matiluḷ mēṭi, vaḷitaṭi ākiya poruḷkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kōṭṭi vaḷimatil ātalāl, ataṉic cūḻa aminta akaḻ vaḷintu kiṭappatu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
pari eṉpatu cūḻnta matilik kuṟippatāki, ataṉ mēl aminta mēṭiyip parikam eṉpatu kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. akam eṉpatu ammeṉat tokuttal vaḻakkam. ataṉpaṭi pariyakam parikam āṉateṉka!
u eṉpatu uyarvu kāṭṭum vēr. uppu, upputal, uvaṇam eṉpavi uyarvu kāṭṭuvaṉa. avvāṟē uppariki upparikam eṉpavi matiṉmēl allatu māṭimēl māṭiyik kuṟippatāy amintaṉa.
pariki eṉpatu vaḷiyum taṉmiyākaliṉ vaḷitaṭiyip parikam eṉal poruntuvatē; iṉip pariki eṉpataṟkum akaḻ, matiluṇ mēṭi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum parivu kūrtalām (aṉpuṟutalām) taṉmiyum kūṟappaṭutal iṇittuk kāṇattakkaṉa.
pari eṉpataṟkup parimaram eṉa oruporuḷ uḷḷami muṉṉark kaṇṭōm. ataṉ vaḷarccic collākiya parikam eṉpatu ataṉ vaḷarccip poruḷukkum uriyatāka viḷaṅku kiṉṟatu. aḵtu eḻumaram eṉpatām.
parimaram ākiya atu parikam eṉpatiyaṉṟik kaṇiyamaram, eḻumaram eṉavum vaḻaṅkum,
eḻumaram kaṭukkum tāḷtōy taṭakki	 eṉpatu puṟam (90).
ivveḻumaram kōṭṭik kataviṉ uṭpuṟattē kuṟuk kākap pōṭappaṭum valiyamaram. eḻūut tāṅkiya katavu eṉpatoru puṟappāṭṭu (97). parikam eṉa ōr iruppup paṭik kalam uṇṭeṉa urikkum.
parikalam eṉpatu uṇkalamākap payaṉpaṭum vāḻiyili; kuruttāka aṟuttu ataṉ vaḷivi virittup pōṭutal vaḻakkāka iruntamiyiṉ parikalam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. parikalak kuruttu eṉṟār cēkkiḻār (periya. appūti. 27).
paric cantam:
cantam eṉpatu naṟumaṇam, amiti, aḻaku iṉimi mutaliya poruḷtarum col. ittakiya polivellām tiruviḻāvum peruviḻāvumām aracaviḻā. teyva viḻākkaḷil cūḻa varutal kaṇkūṭu. ātalāl pariccantam eṉpatu araca paricciṉṉaṅkaḷik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
vīcuveṇ cāmarāti pariccantam muḻutum viṭṭār
eṉpatu mērumantira purāṇam (1048).
paricciṉṉam:
paricciṉṉam eṉpatu araca ciṉṉam. avi kuṭiyum kōlum ūrtiyum piṟavumāy aracaṉic cūḻa amintum vantum polivuṟuttum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷām.
maṇimuttiṉ paricciṉṉam varampiṉṟākak 	
kāṭṭukiṟatu periyapurāṇam (tiruñāṉa. 1016).
* parivāram kāṇka.
paricciṉṉam eṉpatu niṉittavi niṉittapaṭi vikkap paṭupavi yalla! iṉṉavi iṉṉa aḷavu, iṉṉa vakittu eṉa varampu kaṭṭappaṭṭu varuvatu allatu amivatām. ataṉāl paricciṉṉam eṉpatu aḷaviṭṭatu allatu aḷavu paṭṭatu eṉṉum poruḷ peṟṟatu.
paricciṉṉa ñāṉam pariya
eṉpatu civañāṉa muṉivar neñcuviṭu tūtu (81).
paricceṇṭu:
ceṇṭu veḷi eṉpatu kutiri vaṭṭamiṭum veḷiyik kuṟikkum. ceṇṭittal eṉpatu tuḷḷiyōṭal eṉṉum poruḷatu. iṉṟum ceṇṭittal apporuḷil nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaḷipantip pōṭṭut tuḷḷit tuḷḷiyāṭutal paricceṇṭu eṉappaṭṭatām. avvāṭṭam niṉṟukoṇṭu āṭuvatum cuṟṟi vantu āṭuvatumeṉa iruvakittātal periyapurāṇattāl aṟiya varukiṉṟatu.
nilicceṇṭum paricceṇṭum vīcimika makiḻveyti
eṉpatu atu (cēramāṉ. 126).
paricakam:
vīṭu, maṉi, māḷiki eṉpavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa amippil taṉiyē viḷaṅkumāṟu kalikkūṭaṅkaḷi amittal vaḻakkam. ataṉi innāḷ kāṭcik kūṭa amippukaḷāka amippavaṟṟiyum koṇṭu tīrmāṉikka muṭiyum. cuṟṟi varutaṟkēṟṟa vaḷivāṉa vaṭivu kalikkūṭa amippukku uriyatu eṉpatit teḷintu ataṟkup paricakam eṉap peyariṭṭup pōṟṟiyami viḷakkamākiṉṟatu. viḷakkuvatu tirukkōviyār (78).
paṭiccata mākkum paṭamuḷavō num paricakattē
eṉpatu atu.
ippoḻutu paricakameṉap puttāṭci oṉṟum naṭantu varukiṉṟatu. entac cūti oḻikka vēṇṭum eṉṟārō tiruvaḷḷuvar avar peyariyum avar kuṟaḷiyum ulāp pōka viṭṭuviṭṭu antac cūtu vaḻippaṭṭatām paricuccīṭṭi aracē naṭatti varuvatu pāḻil pāḻām ceyki. ap paricuc cīṭṭu viṟpaṉiyakam paricakam eṉac cila iṭaṅkaḷil paḷicciṭukiṉṟatu. paricu kulukkal, paricu vaḻaṅkal viḻākkaḷum pariya pariya talikaḷ talimiyil nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa!
iruppati yellām iḻantālum, ētō oṉṟu viḻuntāl viḻuntatu paricutāṉē!
paricu:
paricam, paricil, paricu eṉpavi koṭi vakiyāl oṉṟāyiṉum peṟuvār nili, peṟum vaki ivaṟṟāl vēṟupāṭu uṇmi karuti mūvaṭivuṟṟatām. itu muṉṉarē eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
oru peṇṇukkut talivaṉ tarum kiyuṟi vēṟu. atu avaḷ kiyil piṟaraṟiyā vaṇṇam cērppatu; tōḻiyum avaḷum uyirōraṉṉar ākaliṉ avaḷ kiyil vaḻaṅkit talivi kiyil cērppippatum kiyuṟiyē. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟāṉatu paricam eṉpatu veḷippaṭa viḷaṅkum.
ūravarkkum urimiyarkkum aṟivittu, avarkaḷ cūḻntuḷḷa aviyattil allatu maṉṟattil maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaḷukku vaḻaṅkum koṭiyē paricamām. akalat tālam oṉṟilē maṅkalap poruḷkaḷ viḷaṅka maṟṟit tālaṅkaḷilē maṇamakaḷukkum avaḷ vīṭṭārukkumām koṭip poruḷkaḷ ūrvalamāyc cūḻa vantu avaṟṟi vaḻaṅkutalum - av vaḻaṅkutalum kuttuviḷakku vittu ataṉic cūḻap parappip palarum pārkka vittalum ākiyavaṟṟi nōkkuvār - pariciyum aṟivār vaḷivup poruḷiyum aṟivār.
iṉi ikalip peṟum paricum, ikalāmal -  pōṭṭi yiṭāmal - peṟum paricilum paṇṭu nikaḻnta pāṉmiyi aṟiyiṉ ip peyarp poruttam viḷaṅkum. kali vallā riyum pulamiyariyum yāṉi mēl vittu ulā varac ceyvatum, avarkaḷukkuriya koṭip poruḷkaḷiyum uṭaṉ ulāvarac ceyvatum, avaṟṟit tālattilē vittu vaḻaṅkiyum ceyta cīrmiyē paricu, paricil eṉṉum peyarkaḷi vaḻaṅ kiṟṟām. paricam pōṭutal, paricil vāḻkki, paricil kaṭānili, paricil nili, paricil viṭi, paricil tuṟi, paricilar, paricilāḷar eṉṉum coṟkaḷellām mēṟkuṟitta muccoṟkaḷiṉ vaḻivantu poruḷilakkaṇap poruḷāyc ciṟakkiṉṟaṉa. ākaliṉ avaṟṟit taṉittaṉi viḷakka vēṇṭiyatilliyām.
paricu koṭip poruḷ tarutalāṉapiṉ taṉmi, allatu kuṇam eṉṉum poruḷum taruvatāyiṟṟu. koṭiyāl pukaḻ varutaliṉ pukaḻ eṉṉum poruḷum ataṟku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paricu perumip paṭuttuvatām vakiyāka amintamiyāl vaki eṉṉum poruḷum tantatu. paricam eṉpatu vaṭṭap peruntaṭṭāka aminta ōṭam ākaliṉ paricukkum ap poruḷ kaṇṭaṉar.
takkaṉum ekkaṉum tamparicu aḻiya (tiruvā. 13:15)  itu pukaḻ.
piḷḷi paricu itu eṉṟāl (periya. tiru. 33.2)  itu taṉmi.
niṉinta apparicē ceyya (periyapu. meyp. 15)  itu vaki.
paricu = ciṟṟōṭam ce.pa.ka. akarāti.
paricu, perumip poruḷ kuṟitta piṉṉar, paricu keṭutal eṉpatu cīraḻivik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. paricu keṭṭavaṉ(ḷ) parici keṭṭavaṉ(ḷ) eṉpavi vaḻaṅkumoḻikaḷ.
paricu peṟuvār ōr oḻuṅkoṭum paṇivoṭum peṟuvarākaliṉ apparicukku oḻuṅku. vaḻi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa.
paricoṭum paravip paṇivār
eṉpatu tēvāram (612.3).

parici:
paricu parici eṉa aikāram peṟum. atu veṇkoṟṟak kuṭi mutalām veṟṟi virutukaḷik kuṟikkum.
parici eṉpataṟkuk kēṭayam eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭu.
nīṭiya pariciyē māvaṭṭaṇam neṭiya vaṭṭam eṉpatu cūṭāmaṇi 				nikaṇṭu (7:18).
valayaṅkaṟ parici eṉpatu irāmāyaṇam     (uyutta. 1323, pā.vē.).
parici, kēṭayam ākaliṉ ataṉip piṭippavaṉ paricik kāraṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. kuṭi piṭippōṉum avvāṟē peyar peṟṟāṉ.
pariñcu:
pariñcu eṉpatu iruporuḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. parintu eṉṉum col pariñcu eṉap pōli vaṭivam peṟutal oṉṟu. parital aṉpu vaḻippaṭṭatākaliṉ vaḷitaṟ poruḷātal teḷivu.
pariñcu iṟi yaḻuṅku kiṉṟēṉ
eṉpatu irāmāyaṇam (uyutta. 2484, pā.vē.).
iṉṉoru poruḷ vāḷiṉ piṭi eṉpatu. vāḷiṉ piṭi vaḷivātal aṟika.
parittal:
parittal eṉpatu cūḻtal, poṟuttal, pūṇutal, kaṭṭutal mutaliya poruḷkaḷit tarum.
puṇṇumiḻ kuruti parippak kiṭantōr
eṉṉum akappāṭal (31) puṇcori kuruti, tammic cūḻak kiṭantōr eṉṉum poruḷ tarutal kāṇka.
pukaḻiyār parikkaṟ pālār eṉṉum kampar vākku poṟuttal pūṇutal ākiya poruḷatu (kiṭ. 346). maṇṭamarp parikkum mataṉuṭi nōṉtāḷ eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭum (75) tāṅkutal allatu poṟuttal poruḷatē.
uyirppiṭi parippa	
 eṉpatu kaṭṭutal poruḷatu (kampa. kiṭ. 864).
parital:1
virumputal, cārtal, varuntutal, añcutal, pirital, akalutal, aḻital mutaliya poruḷkaḷiyum ivaṟṟiṉ cārpāṉa poruḷkaḷiyum virivākak kūṟuvatu paritalākum.
parital = aṉpu; avvaṉpu virumputalām; cārtalām; avvaṉput taṭikku varuntutal nikaḻum; añcutalumām; aṉpiṉ akaṟci pirivām; peruntuyarām; aḻivumām ellām vaṭṭamiṭa amiyum aṉpup parivi viḷakkiṉ peruvirivām.
parital:2
parital = veḷippaṭutal; tōṉṟutal.
katir īṉal eṉpatu potuvaḻakku. katir īṉutalip (payir) parital eṉpatu mukavi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. katir pariyum nēram ippoḻutu maḻi peytāl poṭṭu utirntu pōkum eṉpar.
parital:3
virintu ōṭutalip parital eṉpatu teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku. ōṭip parital eṉṟu iṇittum kūṟuvar. virinta celavuṭiya kutiri pari eṉappaṭuvatum, virintōṭum pānaṭi paripā eṉappaṭuvatum eṇṇattakkavi.
pariti:
pariti eṉpatu vaṭṭa vaṭivu eṉkiṟatu tivākaram.
valayam nēmi tikiri maṇṭilam
pariti āḻi pāṇṭil viruttam
kōḷaki kaṭakam pāliki kommi
taṭṭu valiyam cakkaram cilli
vaṭṭa vaṭiviṟ koṭṭiya peyarē
eṉavum,
kuṭilam taṭṭu vāṅkal kulāval
kōṭṭam vaṇarē vaḷāval vaḷital
eṉavum vaṭṭa vaṭivam kuṟikkum coṟkaḷit tokut turikkiṟatu.
oḷi eṉpatip paritiyam pariti ottāṉ eṉṉum atē nūl. vēḷvit tīyic cuṟṟiyiṭappaṭum taruppip pulli pariti eṉṟu kuṟittal, pācili nāṇaṟ paṭuttup pariti vittu eṉṉum nācciyār tirumoḻiyāl viḷaṅkum (6:7).
pariti katirōṉ ātalāl, ataṉ maṇṭalam pariti vaṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
veṅkatirp pariti vaṭṭam
eṉpatu periya tirumoḻi (4.5:10), paritivāṉavaṉ eṉak katirōṉik kuṟittār kampar (pāla. 346). pariti eṉpatu vaṭṭattiṉ cuṟṟaḷavik kāṭṭutal kaṇakkiyal. pariti eṉpār oruvar tirukkuṟaḷ uriyāciriyar.
ivaṟṟiyaṉṟik kuṇṭalam, paruti, paṭaliki, malli, vaṭṭaṇi, valli, vaṭṭu, vaṭṭaki iṉṉa coṟkaḷum vaṭṭa vaṭivu kuṟippaṉavē. katirōṉ vaṭivu vaṭṭam. atu paritiyañ celvaṉ eṉac cāttaṉārāl collappaṭukiṟatu (maṇimē. 4:1).
tēṉ kūṭṭic collum nakkīrar paritiyiṉ toṭutta taṇkamaḻ alar iṟāl eṉkiṟār (tirumuruku. 299, 300) tēṉaṭiyum katirum pariti vaṭivātal kāṭṭum uvami itu.
tēr uruḷiyum, āḻippaṭiyām cakkarappaṭiyum pariti eṉappaṭum.
attērppariti eṉpatu kaḷavaḻi (4). paritiyil tōṭṭiya vēli eṉpatu kallāṭam (80). pariti katirōṉik kuṟittalāl ataṉpiṉ ataṉ oliyiyum ataṉic cūḻa aminta oḷivaṭṭattiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. parivēṭam eṉpati vaḷintu koḷḷum pariti eṉṉum irakuvamcam.
parippu:
parippu eṉpatu iyakkap poruḷatu. vaṭṭa vaṭivām oṉṟu iyaṅkutal parippu eṉappaṭum. vaṭṭa vaṭivām uruḷik kaṇṭatē (uruḷ = cakkaram) pōkkuvaravuk kālkōḷ. kaṭṭi vaṇṭi mutal kaṭitu paṟakkum vāṉavūrti kāṟum kāluruḷ vaḷarcciyē. aḵtu iyaṟkiyiṉ koṭi. katirum matiyum piṟavum vaṭṭa vaṭivāyiruttalum avi iṭiyīṭilātu iyaṅkalum tūṇṭalām. ataṉi nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntē parippuc colli vaḻaṅkiṉar nam muntiyōr. 
ceññāyiṟṟuc celavum aññāyiṟṟup parippum
parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum
vaḷitiri taru ticiyum
vaṟitu niliiya kāyamum eṉṟivi
ceṉṟaḷan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
iṉitteṉ pōrum uḷarē
eṉṉum puṟappāṭal (30) annāḷit tamiḻvāṇar viṇṇiyaṟ kalit tērcciyi iṉitu viḷakkum.
ceññāyiṟṟiṉatu vītiyum, aññāyiṟṟiṉatu iyakkamum, avviyakkattāl cūḻappaṭum pār vaṭṭamum, kāṟṟiyaṅkum tikkum, ōr ātāramum iṉṟit tāṉē niṟkiṉṟa ākāyamum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa ivaṟṟi āṇṭāṇṭup pōy aḷantu aṟintavarkaḷip pōla nāḷum ittuṇiyaḷavi uṭiyaṉa eṉṟu collum kalviyi uṭiyōrum uḷar eṉavarum ip pakutiyiṉ paḻiyavuri karutat takkatu.
paripāṭal:
teyvamum kāmamum poruḷāka varuvatu paripāṭal; paripāṭṭāl aminta toki, paripāṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. atu eṭṭut tokiyuḷ aintāvatu.
paripāṭal uṟuppukaḷuḷ ciṟanta oṉṟu arākam. uruṭṭu vaṇṇam arākam toṭukkum eṉpār tolkāppiyar (cey. 230) muṭukic cellum uruḷeṉa muṭuku naṭiyiṭalāl paripāṭal eṉappaṭṭatām. ataṉāl uruṭṭu vaṇṇatti aṭuttē muṭuku vaṇṇattiyum vitta tolkāppiyar,
muṭuku vaṇṇam, aṭiyiṟan tōṭi ataṉō raṟṟē eṉṟār (cey. 231). uruḷal, vaḷital poruḷatē yaṉṟō?
paripuram:
paripuram eṉpatu cilampup poruḷatu. puram, puri eṉpaṉavum vaḷitaṟ poruḷāl aminta coṟkaḷē! kāṟpaṭat toṭu vaḷintu kiṭantu oli ceyyum cilampu paripuram eṉappaṭṭatu, vaṭivu karutiya ākkam. cilampu eṉṉum peyar olittaṟ poruḷ karutiya ākkam.
paripuram cilampa eṉkiṟār kampar (pāla. 872, 908).
parimaṇi:
parimaṇi eṉpati maruttuva nūlkaḷ karanti eṉṟu kūṟum. ikkaranti koṭṭik karanti eṉpatu. koṭṭāṅ karanti eṉavum atu vaḻaṅkum (aiṅkuṟu. 26, u.vē.cā. kuṟippu).
karantip pūviṉ amippip puṟappāṭal (261) uvamiyāl kāṭṭum. nāku muliyaṉṉa naṟumpūṅ karanti eṉpatu atu.
nākiṉatu muli eḻuntu kāṭṭātu mēlē parantu kāṭṭuvatu pōlak karantip pūvum koṭiyiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu nillātu ataṉōṭē paṭintu virintu kāṭṭuvatu kaṇkūṭu eṉpār pērāciriyar auvi (puṟam. 261).
parimāṟṟu:
parimāṟṟu koṭuttu vāṅkutal poruḷatu. paṇṭa māṟṟu eṉpatu parimāṟṟamē. oruporuḷ itaṉik koṭuttup piṟitoṉṟaṉiṭam iruntu itaṉip peṟṟatu eṉak kūṟum ōr aṇivaki parimāṟṟaṇi eṉappaṭum. paṇṭa māṟṟu eṉpatu pōl ip parimāṟṟaṇi amivatām. itu parivaruttaṉi eṉavum paṭum. māṟāṭṭu eṉpatum atu.
ivikoṇ ṭiviyeṉak kīntaṉa reṉṟu
navitīra moḻivatu navilpari māṟṟam	- vīracō. 153
eṉpatu ataṉ ilakkaṇam.
parimāṟutal:
parimāṟutal iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷilum peruka vaḻaṅkum col. viruntu parimāṟutal, parimāṟupavar parimāṟal eṉpavi tirumaṇa viḻāttōṟum paṉṉūṟu muṟi paḻakum col. vaḷintu vaḷintu - vaḷiya vaḷiya vantu vantu - parimāṟum. ipparimāṟutalik kuḻal tuḷikaḷil kuḻantik kaṇṇaṉ ciṟuviral taṭavip parimāṟutalākac cuvi coṭṭac coṭṭap pāṭukiṟār periyavar āḻvār.
ciṟuviralkaḷ taṭavip parimāṟa eṉpatu atu (3.6:8). ipparimāṟutal col oḻukutal, ulāvutal, celaviṭutal mutaliya poruḷkaḷāy virikiṉṟatu. parimāṟutal paṇimāṟutalākak kāṭṭukiṟatu ñāṉavāciṭṭam. tuṇikkavari parimāṟa eṉpatu atu (līli. 22).
pariyakam:
pariyakam nūpuram pāṭakam cataṅki
ariyakam kāluk kamivuṟa aṇintu
eṉap pariyakam kālaṇiyātalik kuṟittār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷār (cilap. 6: 84,85). pariyakam kāṟcavaṭi eṉṟār arumpata uriyāciriyar,
pariyakamāvatu,
poṉṉitaḻ potinta paṉṉiṟa maṇivaṭam
piṉṉiya toṭariṟ peruviral mōtiram
taṉṉoṭu toṭakkit tamaṉiyac cilampiṉ
puṟavāy cūḻntu puṇaravip patuvē
eṉṉi?
avvāy makarat taṇikiḷar mōtiram
pivāy pacumpoṟ pariyaka nūpuram
moymmaṇi nāviṉ mulliyaṅ kiṇkiṇi
kuviya ēṉavuṅ kāluk kaṇintāḷ!
eṉṟār eṉak kūṟiṉār aṭiyārkku nallār.
parivaṭṭaṇi:
ici eḻuvum eṇvakiyuḷ oṉṟu parivaṭṭaṇi.
paṇṇal parivaṭṭaṇi ārāytal tivaral
kaṇṇiya celavu viḷiyāṭṭuk kiyūḻ
naṇṇiya kuṟumpōk keṉṟu nāṭṭiya
eṇvakiyāl ici yeḻīi
eṉpatu cilampu (7:5-8).
parivaṭ ṭaṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇam tāṉē
mūvaki naṭiyiṉ muṭiviṟ ṟāki
valakki iruviral vaṉappuṟat taḻīi
iṭakki viraliṉ iyiva tākat
toṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiyum tōṉṟā tākiyum
naṭiyoṭu tōṉṟum nayatta tākum
eṉṟār arumpata vuriyāciriyar.
parivaṭṭam vaḷivup poruḷatātal pōlak kiviral vaḷintiyalalum muṭukalum parivaṭṭaṇip peyarkku uriyavi āyiṉavām.
pari = viri; vaṭṭaṇi = vaṭṭam; vaṭṭaṇi, cuṟṟi vantu naṭañ ceytalik kuṟittal (maṇimē. 7:43) virintu curuṅkum iyalpāl viraṟpeyar vantataṟika.
kōvikaḷi mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vaṭṭamāka icippatu vaṭṭaṇi yākum. mutalnaṭi allatu muntiya naṭiyil kōvikaḷi ēṟṟu avaṟṟi iraṭṭippatē aṭutta naṭi. eṉavē, mutal naṭik kōvikaḷi ēṟṟu mīṇṭum iraṭṭittu olippatē parivaṭṭaṇi (paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil iciyiyal, 214).
parivaṭṭam:
parivaṭṭam, parivēṭam eṉpatiyum kuṟikkum. (parivēṭam kāṇka). parittam eṉpatu parivaṭṭamātali veḷḷi viḻāp pērakarāti cuṭṭum. tirukkōyilkaḷil parivaṭṭam kaṭṭutal iṉṟum kāṇum peruvaḻakku. uyarnta paṭṭāṭiyit taliyic cūḻat toṅkaluṭaṉ kaṭṭutal parivaṭṭam eṉappaṭum. kōyil varavēṟpu maṅkala nikaḻvākap peruntakkārkkuc ceyvatāy amikiṉṟatu. munti maṉṉarkaḷ kālattil aluval aṭiyāḷamākavum parivaṭṭam tikaḻntuḷḷatu. iḻappuk kaṭaṉāka moṭṭiyaṭippavarkkup parivaṭṭam kaṭṭutal peruvaḻakkāka iṉṟum uḷḷatu. vēṭṭi tuṇṭu kaṭṭutal eṉpatu an nikaḻvukkup peyar. uṟaviṉar (campantikaḷ) ceyvatu atu. uṟṟār (paṅkāḷikaḷ) ceytal vilakkuṭiyatu.
parivaruttam:
paripulamputal pōlvatu itu. mīḷa mīḷa varum miku varuttattik kuṟippatuṭaṉ ulaka muṭivu, ūḻi muṭivu eṉpatiyum kuṟikkum.
oruporuḷ koṭuttu iṉṉoru poruḷ vāṅkutal parivaruttam eṉavum paṭum. iṉic cuṟṟutal, vaṭṭam, āmi mutaliya poruḷkaḷiyum tarum.
parivāram:
parivāram eṉpatu pariyāḷam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. pariyāḷam eṉpatu parivāra mākum eṉpatu tivākaramum (makkaṭ) piṅkalamum (840). parivāram cūḻvōrik kuṟikkum. paricaṉar eṉṟatum itu. paṭiñar ēvalar eṉpāriyum kuṟikkum (caturakarāti).
tirukkōyil tiruttoṇṭu mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷukkum parivārap peyar vaḻakkil uṇṭu.
kōvi, tirucci, maturi māvaṭṭat toṭṭiyak kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷukkup parivāram eṉṉum peyaruṇṭu eṉpatic ce.pa.ka. akarāti cuṭṭum. parivāram, uṟi, cūḻvōr, paṭi eṉṉum yāḻppāṇa akarāti.
parivu:
parivu aṉpup poruḷatu. kaṇṇappaṉ oppatōr aṉpiṉmi kaṇṭapiṉ eṉṉum toṭar kaṇṇappaṉ aṉpik kāṭṭum. avaṉ vaṭiveṉa oṉṟu illiyām; aṉpē avaṉ vaṭivām. pariviṉ taṉmi uruvu koṇṭaṉi yavaṉ eṉkiṟatu patiṉōrām tirumuṟi.
parivutapa eṭukkum piṇṭam nacciṉ
yāṉi pukka pulam pōlat
tāṉum uṇṇāṉ ulakamum keṭumē
eṉap pariviṉ ciṟappi viḷakkum puṟanāṉūṟu (184).
aṉpukeṭak koḷḷum poruḷ eṉa urikkum paḻiya uriyāciriyar parivutava eṉṟōti akkuṭikaṭku varutta mika eṉṟurippārum uḷar eṉkiṟār.
parivēṭṭittu, parivēṭpu:
parivēṭṭittu eṉpataṟkuk cuṟṟi eṉṉum poruḷuṇṭu eṉpatu vivēka cintāmaṇiyāl viḷaṅkum.
parivēṭpu eṉpataṟku vaṭṭap poruḷ uṇmi patiṟṟuppattāl teḷivām. pārvaṟ kokkiṉ parivēṭpu eṉpatu atu (21).
parivēṭaṇam eṉpataṟkuc cūḻutal poruḷuṭi miyic caṅkat tamiḻ akarāti collum. parivēṭapuṭam eṉpataṟku āṟu ōri cērap parivēṭamām eṉa maruttuva akarāti urikkum.
parivēṭam:
ūrkōḷ vaṭṭam eṉpatuvum itaṉ peyar. katiriyum tiṅkaḷiyum cuṟṟiyamittuḷḷa oḷivaṭṭam ūrkōḷ allatu parivēṭamām. tiṅkaḷic cuṟṟiya vaṭṭam kōṭṭi eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. eṭṭak kōṭṭi yiṭṭāl kiṭṭa maḻi; kiṭṭak kōṭṭi yiṭṭāl eṭṭa maḻi eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. aṟivārnta talimakkaḷ taliyic cuṟṟi variyappaṭum vaṭṭam ipparivēṭattuṭaṉ eṇṇat takkatām. uḷḷoḷi māṭciyi viḷakkum puṟavoḷik kāṭci aḵteṉka.
parivēṭa miṭṭatu kol pār eṉṟu kaṇṇaṉ acuvat tāmaṉukkuk kāṭṭalik kāṭṭukiṟatu pārata veṇpā.
parivēṭippu:
parivēṭam eṉpataṉ maṟṟi vaṭivam parivēṭippu parivēṭittal eṉpatum atu.
miṉṉaṇi matiyam kōḷvāy
vicumpiṭi naṭappatē pōl
kaṉmaṇi yumiḻum pūṇāṉ
kaṭipala kaṭantu ceṉṟāṉ
eṉṉum cintāmaṇiyum (1098), kaṭipala kaṭantu vicumpiṭiyiṟ kōḷkaḷiṭattē yuṟiyum oḷiyaṇinta matiyam ataṉik kiviṭṭu nilattē naṭappatu pōlap pōntāṉ eṉka! kōḷ = parivēṭippumām; vantavarkaḷ cūḻa naṭuvē pōvataṟkuvami eṉṉum nac. uriyum kāṇka!
parivi:
nantiyā vaṭṭam nantiyāvaṭṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum pū parivi eṉappaṭum. āṅkuḷḷa vaṭṭam, vaṭṭi eṉṉum coṟ poruḷi veḷippaṭak kāṭṭuvatu pariviyātal aṟika.
pari > parivu > parivi.
vaṭṭam vaṭṭu vaṭṭi eṉpaṉa vaḷ eṉṉum vēriliruntu varuvaṉa pōṉṟaṉa.
par - paru:
par eṉpatu pariyātali aṟintu ataṉ vaḷivup poruḷiyum kaṇṭōm. par ukaram cērap paru eṉa ātalāl vaḷinta atu tiraḷutal ātali ivaṇ aṟiyalām.
pariti, paruti eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. pariyatu, parumi ākum.
pariti katirōṉik kuṟittal pōl parutiyum katirik kuṟikkiṟatu.
pariyatu kūrṅkōṭṭa tāyiṉum yāṉi eṉṉum kuṟaḷil pariyatu paruvuruvam uṭiyatu eṉpatic cuṭṭukiṟatu. ittakiya coṟkaḷāl, vaḷitaṟ poruḷukkum tiraḷutal poruḷukkum uḷḷa nerukkam pulappaṭum.
paru - paruttal - parumaṉ parumi eṉpavaṟṟip pārtta aḷavāṉē tiraḷutal tiraṭci eṉpavi eḷitil viḷaṅkum.
veppattālō uḷ ūṟukaḷālō uṭalil ōriṭam taṭikkiṉṟatu; at taṭippuc cellac cellat tiraḷkiṟatu; parukkiṟatu. ataṟkup paru eṉṟu peyar colkiṉṟaṉar.
ōriṭattut tōṉṟum ciṟu kaṭṭi paru eṉappaṭṭāl, uṭalē tiraḷutal paruttal, parumaṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. parumi eṉpatu paruppu eṉṉum poruḷilē koṅku vēḷirāl āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
parappum vitirppum paruppum iṉṟi	- peruṅ. 2:4:196
paḷitam peyta paruppiṟ ṟēyvi	- peruṅ. 4:16:19
paru eṉpatu parutta cilantik kaṭṭiyik kuṟippatuṭaṉ, viyarkkuru eṉpatiyum kuṟippati akarātikaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. paruppam, paruppatam eṉpavi maliyik kuṟikkum. paru eṉpatum maliyik kuṟikkum. paru, kaṭalik kuṟittalic cuṭṭukiṟatu veḷḷiviḻāp pērakarāti.


parukkaṉ:
paruttavaṉ, parukkaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. parukkaṉ maṟṟi uyirkaḷil paruttatiyum, poruḷkaḷil paruttatiyum kuṟittal uṇṭu. parumpaṭiyāṉatu, paruvaṭṭāṉatu paruveṭṭāṉatu eṉpaviyum ittakiyaṉa. paruttavaṉ taṭittavaṉ āvaṉ. uṭal taṭippum uḷḷat taṭippum kūṭa paruttatākac collappaṭum. cerukkit talikkaṉam eṉpatilliyā? atu pōla eṉka! meṉmiyaṟṟa poruḷip parukkaṉ eṉpatu, parumi vaṉmi eṉak koṇṭa poruḷiṉ vaḻittām.
parukkutal:
āṭṭukkuṭṭiyip pāl kuṭikkac ceytalip parukkutal eṉpatu āyar vaḻakku. tāṉē tāyi aṭuttup pāl kuṭiyāk kuṭṭiyip pāl kuṭikka vippatu parukkutal ākum. parukac ceytal parukkutal. iv vaḻakku potuvaḻakkākavum kūṟum aḷavil aṟiya vāykkiṉṟatu. mukavi, maturi vaṭṭāraṅ kaḷuṭaṉ tirucci vaṭṭārattil kēṭkum collākavum uḷḷatu.
parukki:
paruttal eṉpatu parukki eṉavum paṭum. parumaṉātal, cōṟṟup parukki, parukkik kal, uruṇṭi eṉpavi parukkip poruḷaṉa.
parukki eṉpatu ciṟukallē; kūḻāṅ kallē eṉiṉum, atu pariyakal uṟuttum tuyariṉum paruvaral (tuyar) mikavuṇṭākkum kallātal aṟiyattakkatu. paral eṉpatu parukkik kal. ceruppiṭiyē paṭṭuk kāli varuttum kalliṉ koṭuñceyal paṭṭārkkē teriyum. ataṉāl pakivari vāṭṭa valla vēntaṉ oruvaṉ, ceruppiṭip paral aṉṉaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
ceruppiṭic ciṟuparal aṉṉaṉ- puṟam. 257
kaṇṇuḷ kuṟuñciṟu paral pukuntu tarum allalic colli muṭiyātu. kaṇvīṅki, imivīṅki mukam vīṅkip paṭumpāṭu paṭṭār aṉṟip piṟar aṟiyār. ataṉāl viḷak keṇṇeyum tāyppālum kalantu kaṇṇuḷ viṭṭu mella nīvip parukki eṭuppar. innāḷil kūṭac ciṟṟūrkaḷil avar uḷar. parukki eṭuttal eṉpatu viṉi; parukki eṭuppār peyar.
apparukki epparukki? paṭṭa iṭattip paṉmaṭaṅku parukka vikkum ataṟkup parukki eṉpatu nalla paṭṭamtāṉ! parukkik kallukkum parukkāṅkal eṉpatu oru vaḻakkup peyar.
arici, maṇi eṉappaṭum. maṇi eṉpataṟkuc ciṟu eṉṉum poruḷ uṇṭu. ciṟumaṇip payaṟu eṉa oru payaṟum uṇṭu. maṇikkuṭal, maṇikkoccam eṉpavi ciṟumi cuṭṭum oṭṭuc coṟkaḷ. aricimaṇi vēka vikkappaṭṭāl ataṉ aḷavil parumaṉ ākiviṭukiṟatu.
eṉṉa uḻittum cōṟṟup parukkikku vaḻiyilli eṉṟu ēṅkuvār paṭum parukkippāṭu perumpāṭē! parukkiyilāk kūḻukkup pōṭa uppilli eṉpatoru vaṟumippāṭṭu. itu taṉippāṭṭu.
parukki taṇṇīr:
	parukki		= 	cōṟu.
	taṇṇīr		= 	cōṟṟu nīr.
parukki taṇṇīr āyiṟṟā? eṉa viṉavuvatu uṇṭu. uṇṭu āyiṟṟā eṉpatē avviṉā. kañci taṇṇīr eṉpatum oru cār ittakiyatē.
ariciyiṉ aḷavu, vēka vēkap paruppatāl parukki āyiṟṟu. paru, paruppu, pariyatu iṉṉavi uruvap parumi kāṭṭuvaṉa. taṇṇīr eṉpatu nīṟṟut taṇṇīr, cōṟṟut taṇṇīr.
parukal:
parukutal, parukal eṉpavi peruvēṭkiyāl nīr kuṭittal; parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvam eṉṉum uvamiyē parukutal eṉpataṉ vēṭkip perukkatti urikkum. parukuvāṉ pōl nōkkutal evvaḷavu parutta nōkku. paruku vaṉṉa vēṭkiyip pakarkiṉṟatu puṟanāṉūṟu (207). parukutal nālvaki uṇtiṟattil oṉṟākum. parukutal eṉpataṟku viḻuṅkutal poruḷ uṇmiyi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kuṟippār (poruna. 104).
parukutal vēṭkiyarkku nīr tarutal ōraṟam; pēraṟam. ataṉāl, nīrtāṉ koṇarntu parukki iḷippi nīkkīrē eṉa āṇṭāḷār ēvukiṉṟār (nācciyār tirumoḻi 13:4).

parukutal:
ārva mītūra nīr kuṭittal parukutal ākum.
* parukal kāṇka.
parucam:
paricam eṉpatu potumakkaḷāl parucam eṉappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. viricam paḻam eṉpatu virucam paḻam eṉpatu pōṉṟatu atu. āṉāl, ipparucam apporuṭṭataṉṟu. kiṇaṟṟiṉ nīrāḻattik kāṇpār ōrāḷ parucam īrāḷ parucam eṉpatu nāṭṭuppuṟa vaḻakkam.
āḷ parucam, āḷ uyaram; nīriṉ nīḷatti viṭuttu akalatti allatu nili uyarattik kuṟittalāl oruvakiyāl parumiyām. ōrāḷ maṭṭam; īrāḷ maṭṭam eṉpavaṟṟil varum maṭṭam tiraḷal poruḷatē. maṭṭam, mattam, mattippu, maṭṭippu, mattu, maṭṭu eṉpavaṟṟi aṟika!
paruñcu:
paruntu eṉpatu paruñcu eṉak kamparāl āḷappaṭukiṟatu. purintu eṉpatu puriñcu eṉak kocciyāka vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkup pōlvatu atu. āyiṉum paru māṟiṟṟilli.
paruñcu iṟi eṉpatu atu (āraṇiya. 956). paruntukaḷiṉ talivaṉām caṭāyu eṉapporuḷ kāṇiṉ ippirippām. paruñciṟi pariya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya caṭāyu eṉiṉ vēṟu pirippām. iruvakiyākavum koḷvār uḷar. (vi.mu.kō.; kamparāmāyaṇa akarāti).
parutti:
paruttalāl vanta peyar parutti. paruttiraḷ pōl tiraḷtalum paḻuttalum veṭittalum pañcu vāy tiṟakka veḷiyēṟutalum piḷantu virintu paṉmaṭaṅku parumiyāy virintu kāṇalum nōkkiṉ paruttip peyarp poruttam mika viḷaṅkum.
parutti tamiḻakattup paḻamporuḷ. nūṟṟalum neytalum paṇṭē ciṟakka naṭanta toḻil. perumpālum attoḻilil makaḷir īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuval aṉṉa eṉavarum puṟappāṭalāl (125) viḷaṅkum. atilum kaṇavaṉi iḻanta kimmi makaḷir attoḻilil īṭupaṭṭaṉar eṉpatum viḷaṅkukiṟatu. āḷil peṇṭir tāḷil ceyta, nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval eṉkiṟatu naṟṟiṇi (353).
kōṭip parutti vīṭuniṟi peyta
mūṭip paṇṭam iṭiniṟintu
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟum (393) potimūṭip pōrēṟi āṭum nāyum āṭum āṭṭattip paṭṭiṉappāliyum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. potimūṭi eṉpatu parutti mūṭiyip potuvakiyilum piṟavaṟṟic ciṟappu vakiyilum cuṭṭum.
parutti eṉpataṟkup pāram eṉa oru peyar uṇṭu eṉpatik kuṟiñcippāṭṭuk kūṟum (92).
paruttip pañcic ceṟittu vitta kuṭuvi, paruttik kuṇṭiki eṉappaṭum.
paruti:
peritu peritu puvaṉam peritu eṉpatu evarum aṟintatu. apperiya puviyum katirōṉi nōkkac ciṟiya tuṇṭam eṉpatu aṟiviyal kaṇippu. parutiyil iruntu citaṟi viḻunta tuṇṭukaḷil oṉṟē puvi eṉiṉ, ittuṇṭaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum koṇṭiruntatum, ittuṇṭaṅkaḷi akalaviṭṭum mikap pēraṇṭamākat tikaḻum apparutiyām katiriṉ parutiyik kaṇittal aritē. ataṉip paruti eṉṟatu ettaku takavup peyar.
pariti, parutiyām vaṭivāl curampala kaṭavum karivāyp paruti eṉat tērāḻiyik kuṟikkiṟatu patiṟṟuppattu (46:8) paruti ñāyiṟu eṉpatu parutip poruḷ veḷippaṭat terivikkum (peruṅkati 1:48:42).
paruntu:
paruttatoru paṟavi eṉṉum kuṟippāl paruntu eṉappaṭṭatu. pariyatu atu eṉṉum poruṭṭāl paruntukkup pāṟu eṉṟoru peyarum ēṟpaṭṭatu.
parutta vaṭivāl paruntu eṉṉum peyarum, karutta vaṇṇattāl karuṭaṉ kaluḻaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, mika uyaramākap paṟattalāl uvaṇam eṉṉum peyarum ataṟku uṇṭāyiṉa.
paruntu ōririyik kaṇṭu vīḻntu iṟaṅkum tōṟṟattik kaṇṭōr paruntiṉ vīḻvu - paruntiṉ vīḻcci - paruntiṉ vīḻkkāṭu - eṉa nūṟpāviṉ poruṇili amitikku oru vāypāṭu kaṇṭaṉar.
paruntu iriyi eṭukkum iḻupaṟiyiyum āṭum āṭṭattiyum, kuttik kutaṟik koṭumippaṭuttutaliyum kaṇṭōr paruntāṭṭam āṭutal eṉṟaṉar.
pallip piṭuṅkip paruntāṭṭam āṭṭi eṉpatoru taṉippāṭṭu.
paruntiṉ vāyamippik kūrntu kaṇṭa mīṉavar paruntuvāyaṉ eṉa, oru mīṉ vakiyik kaṇṭaṉar. ataṉip paṟāḷi vināyakar paḷḷu tōkip paruntu vāyaṉ maṭṭumīṉ eṉap payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatu (15).
taccup paṇiyar uḷḷattup paruntiṉ vāl patintatu. ataṉāl paruntu vālpōl veṭṭip palakiyiṉ mūlip poruttu iṇittalip paruntuvāl eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar.
parital = vaḷital; parintu = vaḷintu. ipparintu paruntu eṉṟum vaṭivu koṇṭu vaḷiyalic cuṭṭiyatu. paṟakkum paruntukkum vaḷiyalām paruntukkum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭa vēṇṭumē eṉṟu eṇṇiya caṅkac cāṉṟōr paṟāap paruntu eṉṟaṉar (kali. 147).
paruppam:
paruppu - paruttatu; paruppu + am = paruppam. am perumip poruḷ; (e-ṭu) kūṭu + am = kūṭam.
parutta uyiri yāṉi; avviyāṉi naṭakkum mali eṉappaṭum. avviyāṉiyiṉ mukapaṭāmum, ampāriyum parumam eṉappaṭṭāl avaṟṟik koṇṭa yāṉiyum, avviyāṉiyoṭu orupuṭi oppākac collappaṭum naṭavā maliyum paruppam eṉappaṭuvatu takavutāṉē! avvakiyāl malikkup paruppam eṉṉum peyarum paruppatam eṉṉum peyarum uṇṭāyiṉa. parvatam eṉpatu vaṭacol. paruppam teṉcol.
nīlap paruppam eṉpatu peruṅkati (5:1:181) paruppatamali eṉṉumoru maliyiyum kāṭṭum peruṅkati (5.3:55).
maliyamali eṉpatu pōlap potuc ciṟappup peyar iṇiyām. patam eṉpatu tiraṇṭatu, parumiyum paruvamum amintatu eṉṉum poruḷatātalum aṟika. aṟiyiṉ paruppatam eṉṉum teṉcol nili teḷivām. patam tiraṭṭappaṭṭatātal veṇṇeykkoru peyaratātalāl kaṇṭu koḷka!
paruppu:
paruppu eṉpatu parumip poruḷatu. paruppuṭip pavaḷam pōla eṉavarum cintāmaṇi ipporuḷit terivikkum (2273).
ayirurup puṟṟa āṭami vicayam
kavavoṭu piṭitta vakiyami mōtakam
eṉa varum maturik kāñcikku (625, 626) paruppum tēṅkāyumākiya uḷḷīṭukaḷōṭē kaṇṭacarukkari kūṭṭip piṭitta vakuppaminta vemmi poruntiṉa appam eṉa uri varikiṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
avarip payaṟṟiṉ paruppuc cōṟṟic colkiṟatu perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi (195). kummāyam eṉpatōr uṇavu kuṟip. u.vē.cā. kuḻiyac camitta paruppu eṉpār maṇavāḷa māmuṉikaḷ (periyāḻvār 3. 3:37) payaṟṟatu kummāyam naṉ. 299 mayili. mēṟ.
paruppu, payaṟṟi uṭittuc ceyyappaṭṭatu. tōl urittu ākkappaṭṭatu. paruppu eṉṉum col, paruppuk koṇṭu arikkappaṭum tuviyalik kuṟittu, piṉṉart tuviyal eṉṉum potup poruḷil virutunakar vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
paruppuk kuḻampu:
cāmpār illāc cāppāṭā? paruppu illāt tirumaṇamā? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum poyttup pōṉatu. cāmpārtāṉ uṇṭēyaṉṟip paruppum illi; kuḻampum illi. iṉṉum puḷikkuḻampu, kārak kuḻampu, vaṟṟal kuḻampu, kaṇṭak kuḻampu ettaṉi vaki. mōrk kuḻampi viṭa muṭiyumā? oru cāmpār eṉṉa vēli ceykiṉṟatu!
parumam:
makaḷiriṉ tiraṇṭa mārpakam parumam eṉappaṭum. kommi, koḻumi eṉpaṉa pōla ataṉiyal viḷakkum peyarām. peṇṭir aḻakeṉa uṟuppilakkaṇam collum nūlkaḷ mārpakap parumi cuṭṭutalum, kōyiṟ ciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟum kāṇpār ippeyaramiti poruntumāṟṟi aṟivar. ivvamippum paruvat tiraṭciyoṭum kūṭiyatu eṉpatum collamippāl koḷḷat takkatām. makaḷir, parutta toṭippuṟam kuṟippatu parumam eṉpatu yāḻppāṇa akarāti.
arippaṭṭikiyik kuṟippatu eṉpatu tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi. atu patiṉeṭṭu vaṭaṅkoṇṭatu eṉṉum parumi cuṭṭiyatu eṉpatu aṟiyaṟpālatu (146) kaḷiṟṟiṉ kaḻuttu metti eṉpatu kalittoki (97) kutiriyiṉ kalaṇi eṉpatu neṭunalvāṭi (179).
parumam, parumi eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. parumaṉ eṉpatum atu. ampāriyip parumakkaṭṭu eṉṟum (uyut. 3360) piṭariyil tavicit tāṅkiya yāṉi, parumayāṉi eṉṟum (ayōt. 2) kamparāl kuṟippiṭappaṭum.
parumal:
parutta marakki parumal eṉappaṭum. paruttatu parumaṉ ātal pōlap parumal eṉappaṭṭatām.
kappalukkuriya kuṟukku marattiṉ ki parumal eṉappaṭum eṉpatic cuṭṭukiṟatu ce.pa.ka. akarāti.
parumitam:
perumitam eṉpatu perumiyākavum taṉṉeṭuppākavum iruniliyil amiyum. ipperumitam parumitam eṉavum paṭum. parumitam makiḻviyum kuṟikkum. parumittal eṉpatu aḻakuṟuttal paṇṇuṟuttal eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟittal parumam eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka varum poruḷām.
muttam parumittiṭi tēyntu	- kūrma. anta. 53
parumitta kaḷiṟṟi ṉāṉum	- cin. kānta. 20
eṉpavaṟṟi maturit tamiḻccaṅka akarāti eṭuttuk kāṭṭum.
parumi:
parumi eṉpatu paruttiruttal, perumi eṉpavaṟṟi veḷippaṭa uṇarttum. paruñcōḷam, paruṅkīri, paruntēkku, parunel, parupporuḷ, parumpaṭi, parumaṭṭam, paruveṭi, paruvuṭal ivvāṟu parumi cuṭṭiya aṭiyāka varuvatu peruvaḻakku. paru peru eṉṟum ākum.
peruntīṉi, perumpāṭu, peruṅkāyam, peruṅkōḻi ivvāṟu parumi perumiyaṭiyāka varutal, parumitam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. paruval eṉpatu parumi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkutal ulaka vaḻakku.

paruvam:
paruvamaḻi eṉpatum paruvattē payircey eṉpatum aṉivarum aṟintavi. paruvam eṉpatu maḻipeyyum paruvamām.
kālaccōḷam, kālapparutti, kālamaḻi eṉpavaṟṟil uḷḷa kālam maḻikkālamātal uḻutoḻilōr naṉkaṟintatu. taṟkālam eṉpatu maḻik kālatti muṉṉē kuṟittateṉpatu taṟpāṭiya taḷiyuṇaviṟ puḷ tēmpa eṉavarum paṭṭiṉap pāliyāl viḷaṅkum (3).
kārē kūtir muṉpaṉi piṉpaṉi iḷavēṉil vēṉil eṉa aṟuvakip paruvamum eṇṇiyiruppatum mutaṟkaṇ kār iṭam peṟṟiruppatum karutattakkaṉa.
karumukil tiraṇṭu vāṉmaṟiya urukkoṇṭu poḻitalip paruvak koṇmū eṉpar. koṇṭal eṉpatum nīr koṇṭa karumukiliyē yām. cūṟkoṇṭa makaḷ makappēṟu uṟuvatupōl cūṟkoṇṭa mukil maḻippēṟu vaḻaṅki ulakatti ōmputal vāṉperuñciṟappām. ipparuvam, iyaṟki mutirntu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum viḻuppamikkatākaliṉ paruvam eṉap peṟṟatām.
kumari orutti iyaṟkiyiṉ vimmutalāl ilvāḻvu ēṟkum paruvatti aṭikiṟāḷ eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟi paruvamaṭitalām. uḻavaṭikkālam kaṟkum kālam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ cīriya - vāytta - poḻutukaḷē allatu kālaṅkaḷē paruvam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatām. atu nikaḻtaṟku allatu atu ceytaṟkut tiraṇṭu niṟkum muḻuttamē paruvam eṉat tērntatum, muḻumati nāḷām muḻuttattil tirumaṇam vittatum ataṟku muḻuttam eṉap peyariṭṭatum muntiyōr paruvam pōṟṟiya tērccic cāṉṟukaḷām.
paruvamāli:
pēti, petumpi, maṅki, maṭanti, arivi, terivi, pēriḷampeṇ eṉappaṭum peṇṭir paruvam ēḻaṉiyum urittu, avvēḻ paruvattum kaṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷa tuṟik kalvic ciṟappukaḷiyum eṭutturittal paruva māli eṉappaṭum.
peṇparuvam
ēḻukkum āṇṭēḻ tuṟikkalvi ēṟṟamuṟal
vāḻparuva māliyā māl	- pira. tira. 10
peṇṭir paruva varuṇaṉi nōkku maṭṭumaṉṟi avartam kalvit tiṟam kuṟittum karutiyiruttal kuṟippiṭat takkatām.
poruntu kalviyum celvamum pūkkum pukaḻ vāḻvukkut takka putuppaṭippākap paruvamāli veḷippaṭal vēṇṭum.
paruvaral:
iṭukkaṇ, paḻaṅkaṇ, puṉkaṇ, tuṉpam, tuyar, iṉṉal, allal eṉṉa eṇṇaṟṟa coṟkaḷ kavalikku uḷḷaṉa. kavalip perukkam vāḻvil uṇmiyik kāṭṭuvatu pōlavē, coṟkaḷum virivāka uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, paruvaral oṉṟu. eñciya tuyarac coṟkaḷiṉum viñciya tuyari viḷakku vatu paruvaral eṉpati ataṉ parumiyē kāṭṭum. paruvarutal eṉiṉum paruvaralē!
paruvaral poṟukka muṭiyāt tuyar eṉpatip poṟi yiṉṟu perukiya paruvaral eṉpatāl veḷippaṭuttuvār tolkāppiyar (1097).
paruvaral tīrak kaṭavu matipāka
eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟup paruvaral perumpiṟiteṉa aṟika (488).
ataṉiṉum paruntuyar illiyē!
paruvi:
paruvi = parutti.
paruvi vittiya pintāḷ puṉam	- peruṅ. 1:50:16
paruvi parīi eṉavum ākum.
parīi vittiya	- kuṟun. 72
vittutal = vitittal.
par - parūu:
parūu parumip poruḷatātalit tivākaramum (paṇpu) piṅkalamum (1928) urikkum.
parūuc cuṭar, parūuc cevi, parūut toṭi iṉṉa pala vaṟṟic colkiṟatu peruṅkati.
parūukkaṇ, parūukkari, parūukkāḻ, parūukkuṭar, parūukkural, parūukki, parūuttāḷ, parūuttiri, parūuppakaṭu, parūuppaṉi, parūuppeyal iṉṉa palavaṟṟip payila vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa pāṭṭu, tokikaḷ.
par - parēr:
paru + ēr = parēr. paruttatum aḻakiyatumām taṉmi parēr eṉappaṭṭatu. parumi aḻaku ivaṟṟoṭum valimiyum amintatu parēr eṟuḻ eṉappaṭṭatu.
parēreṟuḻk kaḻaṟkāl	- paṭ. 294
parēreṟuḻt taṭakki	- akam. 148
parēreṟuḻt tiṇitōḷ	- perum. 90; neṭunal. 31
parēreṟuḻ muḻuvuttōḷ	- patiṟ. 81
eṉpavaṟṟik kāṇka. puḻaku eṉpatoru pū. parēram puḻaku eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu (kuṟiñcip pāṭṭu 96) ataṉpū cenniṟam eṉpatu arakku virittaṉṉa eṉpatāl pulappaṭukiṉṟatu.
palakaṇi:
kaṇi = kaṇṇiyuṭiyatu; kaṇṇāvatu kaṇpōṉṟa tuḷiyamippu. palakaṇi = palavāya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyatum kāṟṟu varu vaḻiyiṉatumākiya vīṭṭu amippu. kāṟṟu varumvaḻi, kālatar. kāl = kāṟṟu. atar = vaḻi. māṉkaṇ kālatar māḷiki eṉpa taṉāl, māṉkaṇ pōṉṟa amippu uṭiyatu eṉpatu viḷakkamām (cilap. 5:8).
* kaṇvāy kāṇka.
palaki:
maraccaṭṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi iḻittu oḻuṅkuṟuttic ceyyappaṭṭa irukki, paṭukki ākiyavi palaki eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
aṭṭil pakutiyil amarntu paṇi ceyya maṇippalaki payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cuvarppūccukkuṭ tēyppup palaki payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. ciṟitāka appalaki iruntamiyāl maṇippalaki eṉappaṭṭatu. maṇi ciṟumip poruḷatu. e-ṭu: maṇikkaṭali; maṇikkayiṟu; maṇikkuṭal.
naṉcey nilattil kuḻimitittuc cēṟākkiya piṉ oppuravu ākkuvataṟku māṭu pūṭṭiyō āḷkaḷ iḻuttō palaki yaṭittal allatu palaki taṭaval vēḷāṇ vaḻakku.
caṅkaccāṉṟōr amarntu āynta irukki palaki eṉap paṭṭatu. caṅkap palaki eṉpatu atu. karumpalaki paḷḷikaḷiṉ cāṉṟu; carappalaki, kaṭikaḷiṉ cāṉṟu; kuḻantikaḷiṭam kaṟpalaki. tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil palakiyiṭṭa paṭalam eṉpatu oṉṟu. innāḷ viḷiyāṭalkaḷil oṉṟu maṭṭip palaki viḷiyāṭṭu.
pulavarkaḷ irunta irukkiyi aṉṟi avarkaḷ irunta iṭamum palaki eṉa vaḻakkūṉṟiyamiyē innāḷiṉ aṟamaṉṟap palaki (Court Bench) mūlamām.
eṇṇey nīrāṭac ceṉṟa tiricirapuram makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭci cuntaram piḷḷi irukitāṉē irukkiṟatu eppaṭi nīrāṭuvatu eṉa viṉāvip palaki varacceytu nīrāṭiṉār eṉpatu cevivaḻicceyti.
nīrnilikaḷil cella akaṉṟa palakikaḷip paricalākap payaṉpaṭuttiyatu uṇṭu. attuṟi parical tuṟi. irumput takaṭum palaki eṉṟāki iruppup palaki eṉappaṭṭatu.
maruppiṭip payiṉṟa mācaṟu maṇittoḻil
parappami palaki	- peruṅ. 1:38:147-8
vaṭṭikip palaki	- peruṅ. 1:38:169
vaṭṭikip palaki = ōviyam eḻututaṟkuriya palaki.
arakkuviṉip palaki	- peruṅ. 1:48:61
paravit taṭṭiṟ paṉmaṇip palaki	- peruṅ. 1:48:16
poṟinili aminta ceṟinilip palaki	- peruṅ. 2:6:72
mutaṉilip palaki (ariyaṇi)	- peruṅ. 2:6:101
taṇṇitip palaki (poṟpalaki)	- peruṅ. 2:18:41
maṇikiḷar palaki	- peruṅ. 4:15:105
eṉap palaki peruṅkatiyil payila vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
marattālākiya palaki, palavaki aṇimaṇi vēlip pāṭaminta irukkiyāy ūrtiyāyk katavāyc cuvarāyk karuviyāy virinta vaki itu.
palavaṟṟi oṭṭiya oṭṭup palaki, kalnārp palaki, muṟi maṉṟap palaki putuvaravāṉālum paḻampalakik koṭi yēyām.
innāḷil palavaṟṟi oṭṭāmal iṇikkāmal pāḷamāka aminta ūñcal, kaṭṭil eṉappaṭuvaṉa orē takaṭāka iruppiṉum paḻampalakip peyari viṭṭila. pala oṉṟāṉālum, oṉṟu pala eṉap peyar peṟalum kālantōṟum vaḻaṅkum moḻikkoṭiyām. valluppalaki eṉpatu cūtāṭuvār avvāṭṭattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum palaki. ataṉit tolkāppiyar,
nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
āvayiṉ ukaram keṭutalum urittē
ukaram keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum
eṉkiṟār (eḻuttu. 375). patiṉintām puḷḷi viḷiyāṭṭu, pallāṅkuḻi viḷiyāṭṭu, nāyum puliyum viḷiyāṭṭu, tāya viḷiyāṭṭu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉōṭiyām viḷiyāṭṭu vallu. ataṉkāy vaṭṭu eṉappaṭum. vaṭṭiṉum cūtiṉum vāṉporuḷ vaḻaṅkik keṭṭa kuṭiyiṉaṉ maṇimēkalic cātuvaṉ maṭṭum tāṉā? naḷaṉ eṉṉa, tarumaṉ eṉṉa ellārum vallāṭṭam āṭiyavarē!
kaṇvāy kālvāy ākiyavaṟṟiṉ maṭikaḷip palakiyāl aṭippar. ataṟku ō eṉpatu peyar. ō = matakunīr tāṅkum palaki; kūri vēyvataṟkum paṭappi vēytaṟkum taṭṭi oḻuṅkuṟuttum palaki taṭṭuppalakiyām; pōrkkaḷattil meykkāppākak koḷḷum meymaṟi (kavacam) palaki eṉappaṭṭatu. vaṭimaṇip palaki (perumpāṇ. 120). pōrkkatavu eṉpataṟkup palakikaḷ tammiṟ cērutaliyuṭiya katavu eṉpār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (paṭ. 40). palakiyāl ceyyappaṭṭa cuvar, pākar eṉappaṭṭati avarē kūṟukiṟār (ciṟupāṇ. 258) malivāṇar kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷ palaki aṭippukaḷāl amintami paṇṭu mutal iṉṟuvari kaṇkūṭu. puttulaka nākarikam cuvaraṭippākap palakiyik koḷkiṉṟatu. palakiyum cīppum matiluṟuppu.
palakiyaṭippu:
vīṭṭilum kāṭṭilum poruḷkaḷ pōṭṭu vikkavum kāval karuti amarntirukkavum amikkappaṭuvatu paraṇ ākum. paraṇ eṉpatum paraṇi eṉpatum potuvaḻakku. ataṉip palaki aṭippu eṉpatu aruppuk kōṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. palakiyāl aṭittup parappappaṭuvatāl peṟṟa peyar. maṟṟip paraṇ kampukaḷip parappi amikkappaṭṭatākum.
palam:
palam eṉa oru niṟikal (eṭikal) iruntatu. palam valimip poruḷilum vaḻaṅkum. atu valam eṉpataṉ tiripākum. iṉi māṭu palappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatum payir palaṉ(m) piṭittirukkiṟatu eṉpatum uḻavar vaḻakku ākum. māṭṭut tarakar vaḻakkilum muṉṉatu uṇṭu.
palāppaḻam:
palāppaḻam parutta paḻam. atu, pal - paru - pal - palā = perum paḻam. ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻam eṉpatu kuṟuntoki(18).
palā - palavu. tīñcuḷip palaviṉ paḻamūḻkkumē	- puṟam. 109
palāppaḻam, tamiḻk kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuc ciṟappāka uriyati aṟintiruntum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali vaṭacollākak kāṭṭiyiruppatu viyakkattakka neñcaḻuttam eṉkiṟār pāvāṇar. (vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu pak. 202)
palici:
palici eṉpatu vaṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷil nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. kalveṭṭukaḷilum iccol ip poruḷil āḷappaṭum. palici eṉpatu ilēcu (ūtiyam) eṉṉum poruḷil cintāmaṇiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu (770). vaṭṭi eṉiṉum, ūtiyam eṉiṉum eḷimiyāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttiṉatu atu.

paluku:
nilattik kiḷaṟik kaḷi peyarkkap payaṉpaṭuttum palukuc caṭṭam uḻavar karuvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. atu palapalkaḷik koṇṭu iruttalāl palaku, paluku eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu. puḻuti puraṭṭi eṉappaṭum. nel muḷittu nāṉku aintu viral uyaramākap payir irukkum pōtu palukaṭittal naṭimuṟi vaḻakkākum.
pal:
paṟṟu eṉpatu pal eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka vanta col. pal eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉa?
pal eṉpatu veṇmi kuṟitta col. pala pala eṉa viṭintatu eṉṉum vaḻakkilum pāl eṉṉum collilum veṇmip poruḷ uḷḷatātal aṟika! pal, pāl eṉpavi pōlak kal, kāl eṉpavi karumip poruḷ tarutaliyum oppiṭṭuk kāṇka.
pal eṉpatu paḷ eṉat tirintum veṇmip poruḷ tarutalip paḷapaḷa, paḷapaḷappu paḷicciṭal eṉpavaṟṟilum paḷikku paḷiṅku eṉpavaṟṟilum kāṇka. ivvāṟē kal eṉpatu kaḷ eṉat tirintu karumip poruḷ tarutalik kaḷam, kaḷavu, kaḷar, kaḷḷam, kaḷvaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷik koṇṭu teḷivu ceytu oppiṭṭuk koḷka!
veṇmip poruḷ tarum pal piṉṉarp paṉmip poruḷ taruvatāy virintatu eppaṭi?
uṭaṟpoṟikaḷi mey, vāy, kaṇ, mūkku, ceviyeṉa eṇṇuvar. ivaṟṟuḷ vāyiṉ uḷḷuṟuppukaḷil oṉṟākiyatu pal, itaṉip perukkal vāypāṭu pōla, eṇṇāṉku muppattiraṇṭu paṟkāṭṭi eṉkiṟatu oru taṉippāṭṭu.
kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭu; kātukaḷ iraṇṭu; āṉāl paṟkaḷō muppattiraṇṭu. aviyum kaṇkaḷ kātukaḷ pōlat taṉit taṉi niṉṟaṉa alla, toṭuttup pattiyāy amintuḷḷaṉa. mulli yarumpiyō muttiyō kōttu vitta kōvi eṉṉa uvamit toṭi paṭak kūṟappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉa. ippalliṉ variciyē pala veṉpati allatu paṉmiyi vaḻaṅkum kuṟiyāyiṟṟām.
uḻavuk karuvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu palakuc caṭṭam; ataṉip payirūṭu aṭittalip palakaṭittal eṉpar. palaku, paluku eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. kaḷi kutti pōl pal pallākiya muḷikaḷi yuṭiyatu appalaku eṉpati aṟipavar, ataṉ peyarp poruḷit teḷivāka aṟivar. palakaṭittalip palliyāṭutal eṉpatu paṇṭiyōr vaḻakku.
pūḻi mayaṅkap palavuḻutu vittip palli yāṭiya
eṉpatu puṟappāṭṭu (120) palliyāṭutalit tāḷiyaṭittal eṉpār puṟanāṉūṟṟu urikārar, ūṭaṭittal eṉpatu ikkāla vaḻakku.
palaku amippit tali cīvum cīppuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu vaṭivamippuk kāṇka! cīppup pal palpōṉa cīppu eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiyum karutuka. koḻuvoṭu cērkkappaṭṭatum paṟkaḷ uḷḷatumāṉa palakiyoṭu aminta kalappi palluk kalappi eṉappaṭutaliyum, ceṅkal kaṭṭaṭa mēl taḷattil oṉṟuviṭṭu oṉṟu veḷiyē nīṭṭi vikkappeṟum ceṅkal varici palluvari eṉappaṭutaliyum eṇṇalām.
īra veṇkāyam, veḷḷip pūṇṭu ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ piḷavukaḷ allatu kappukaḷ paṟkaḷ eṉṟē vaḻakkil iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. pal pallāka eṭuttē naṭavu ceytalum kaṇkūṭāṉatē. ciṟṟūrp palacarakkuk kaṭikaḷil, pūṭu oru palluk koṭuṅkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭu vāṅkutal aṇmik kālam vari naṭimuṟiyil iruntatēyām! ivaṟṟilellām pal taṉ mūlamām oḷipporuḷiyum paṉmip poruḷiyum iḻantu, orumi cuṭṭi vantatām.
muppaḻam vāḻi, mā, palā eṉpavi. ivaṟṟuḷ palā cuḷi eṉappaṭum. kaṉiyiṭi ēṟiya cuḷi eṉṉum pāvēntar vākkil vanta kaṉi palākkaṉi eṉpatu evarum aṟintatē. palāk koṭṭiyiṉ vaṭivi ūṉṟik kāṇpār vaṇṇamum vaṭivamum palloṭu ottiruppatik kāṇpar. ataṉ peyarīṭṭiyum aṟintu koḷvar. pal pōṉṟa koṭṭi yuṭiyatum, ivaṟṟiyum palavākak koṇṭatum palā eṉappaṭṭatām. par pal palā eṉavaral uṇmiyāl ataṟkup parumip poruḷum uṇṭām.
palāc cuḷi eṉpatu pōlavē, paruttic cuḷi eṉpatum vaḻakkē. cuḷ eṉa aṭikkum veyilāl muṟṟi veṭippatāl cuḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. parutti cuḷittirukkiṟatu eṉpatu parutti veṭippik kuṟikkum. paruttic cuḷi eṭuppārkkup paruttiyē kūṟiṭṭuk kūliyākat tarutal vaḻakkam. parutti eṭukkappaṭṭa kolukku (kotukku) palpallāka irukkum. muḷḷip pōl kuttavum ceyyum. ataṉi uṇarntu paruttikkup pal eṉap peyar vittaṉar. atu paṉ eṉavum paṉṉal eṉavum ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu.
miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum
annāṟ collum toḻiṟpeyar iyala
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (345).
paṉṉal vēlip paṇinal lūrē
eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu (345).
parutti, vēlip payirō eṉpārāyiṉ, parutti vēlic cīṟūr eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟu terivikkum (299). innāḷil vēlip parutti eṉa oru parutti vēlip puṟaṅkaḷil iruttal aṟika.
paṉ ākiya paruttiyil iruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa nūl paṉuval eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar, avvāṭi nūlik kuṟitta paṉuval, aṟivu nūlik kuṟittatu.
pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuval iḻiyākac
ceñcol pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu
eṉṟu paṉuvali oppiṭṭup pakarkiṉṟatu naṉṉūl (24).
pal paṉ ākumā? kal kaṉ āṉatē. kaṉmāp palaki eṉac caṅkappalaki vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatē.
palpallāy aminta aṟuvāḷ paṉṉaṟuvāḷ eṉappaṭuvatiyum, ataṉ paṟkaḷik kūrākkuvatip paṉṉu vittal eṉappaṭuvatiyum nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil naṉku aṟiyalām.
palamoḻi vallār paṉmoḻip pulavar eṉappaṭutaliyum palkāl colliyatiyē collutal, paṉṉutal, paṉṉip paṉṉip pēcutal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭutaliyum teḷika!
paṉi eṉṉum tolpukaḻ maratti aṟivōm. teṉṉi, puṉṉi eṉpavi pōlap paṉṉi eṉpatē ataṉ mūtta peyarāka iruntu iṭikkuṟi eytiyirukka vēṇṭum. kumarik kaṇṭa nāḷilēyē ēḻpaṉi nāṭu pēcap paṭutalālum paṉikkoṭiyum paṉampū māliyum cērarkkuriyavi ātalālum avaṟṟit tolkāppiyamum cuṭṭutalālum paṉi eṉṉum vaṭivam mikap paḻamiyē vantuviṭṭatu eṉpatik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. evvaḷavu kālam kaṭantālum cila aṭimūlaṅkaḷ uṇmiyi viḷakki uṟuti ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉavām.
pataṉīri vaṭippataṟkup payaṉpaṭuvatu paṉṉāṭi. atu paṉi maṭalic cūḻntu vali pōl irukkum nār āṭiyik kūṭṭip piṭittu amitta tām. paṉ āṭi paṉṉāṭi āyiṟṟu. iḻi kalakkamāṉa uṭiyiyum neyyariyiyum paṉṉāṭi eṉpatum vaḻakkam! paṉṉāṭiyil amintuḷḷa paṉ paṉṉi eṉpataṉ munti mūlaṅkāṭṭuvatām. teṉṉi ōli teṉṉōli āṉāṟ pōlap paṉṉi āṭi paṉṉāṭi āyiṟṟām. teṉṉavaṉ kūṭalil oru teru teṉṉōlikkārat teru eṉpatu.
paṉikkum pallukkum eṉṉa toṭarpu? paṉiyiṉ aṭimarap parappellām palpallāy - ceṟumpu uṭiyatāy - iruppatu poruḷviḷakkam ceyvatām. irumpaṉañ ceṟumpu eṉṉum akanāṉūṟu (277). ceṟumpu, ciṟāmpu cetumpu eṉa vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa. cimpu eṉpatu kocci vaḻakku.
pal toṭarpāl palli peyar peṟṟatu pōlavē, paṉṟi eṉpatu pal toṭarpāl peyar peṟṟatēyām. pal + ti = paṉṟi. kaṭivāy ōraṅkaḷil neṭum pal allatu kōrip pal irunta kālattil ataṟkuc cūṭṭap peṟṟa peyar paṉṟi. kōriyiṉ kuruttu nīḷal iyaṟkiyi nōkkik kāṇpār kōrippal eṉṉum peyarīṭṭu viḷakkamum kāṇpār. yāṉiyiṉ neṭum pallām kompu tēyāmal nilittatu. paṉṟikkō tēyntoḻintatu. kōrippal, māntarkkum munti yuṭimiyāka iruntatip paḻañciṟpaṅkaḷ paṟiyaṟiya villiyā! paṉṟikku valluḷi eṉṉum peyar uṇmiyiyum, yāṉiyik kuṟikkum kōṭṭumā eṉṉum peyar paṉṟiyiyum kuṟittaliyum aṟiyiṉ, peyariṉ poruḷ viḷakkamām.
pal eṉpatu pattiṉ mūlamām. pal + tu = paḵtu; pattu āyiṟṟu. pal paṉ eṉa ākiyum pattik kuṟikkum. iruki viralkaḷiyum eṇṇat terintu koṇṭa kālattil atuvē palavaṟṟuḷ ucca eṇṇākak karuti vikkappeṟṟa peyarām. paḵtu pattu eṉṉum iruvaki āṭcikaḷum paṇṭi nūlkaḷilē iṭam peṟṟiruttalum paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉpatilē paṉ eṉṉum vaṭivē pattiṉik kuṟittu niṟṟalum eṇṇuka! iṉip paṉṉoruvar eṉap paripāṭal kūṟuvatum (8) paṉṉoṉṟu eṉa arukalāka vaḻakkil uḷḷatum iṇittuk kāṇalām.
iṉip paḵtu pattu ākumō? eṉpār uḷareṉiṉ aḵkuḷ, akkuḷ aḵkam, akkam eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟik karutuvārāka!
perunīrp perukkuṭiya kumarik kaṇṭattōr āṟu, paḵṟuḷi eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu aṟiñar aṟintatu. pala tuḷi eṉpatē paḵtuḷi eṉpatum, pala tuḷi peru veḷḷam eṉpatum evarē aṟiyār?
pala tuḷi paḵṟuḷiyāṉatu pōlavē, pala tāḻici paḵṟāḻiciyākavum, cila tāḻici ciḵṟāḻiciyākavum koccakak kalippā vakiyil iṭampeṟṟami yāppiyal vallār kōppuṟa aṟintaviyām.
paḵṟi eṉpatu paṭakik kuṟikkum col. āṉāl, oru paṭakik kuṟikkāmal pala paṭakukaḷik kuṟikkum tokutic col eṉpatu paḵṟi eṉpataṉ vēr pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. pal + ti = paḵṟi.
pala paṭakukaḷik kuṟippatu paḵṟi eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu uṇṭō eṉiṉ, uṇṭu eṉpatām. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil paṭṭiṉappāliyil maṭṭum orō ōr iṭattil (30) āḷappaṭṭuḷḷa col, paḵṟi atu,
koḻumpalkuṭic ceḻumpākkattuk
kuṟumpallūr neṭuñcōṇāṭṭu
veḷḷiyuppiṉ koḷḷicāṟṟi
nelloṭuvanta valvāyppaḵṟi
paṇinilippuraviyiṉ aṇimutaṟpiṇikkum
eṉpatu. pantiyilē niṟṟaliyuṭiya kutirikaḷip piṇikkumāṟu pōlac cārnta taṟikaḷilē piṇikkum eṉkiṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. vaṇikap paṭakukaḷ palavākac cellutalum mīṇṭu varutalum vaḻakkam eṉpati varalāṟṟu vaḻi aṟintōr, paḵṟiyiṉ meypporuḷ teḷivar. akkālac cāttukaḷ vaṇikar kūṇṭu vaṇṭikaḷ variciyākac ceṉṟu vaṇikam ceytu tirumpiya ceytiyum oppiṭṭuk kāṇat takkatē.
tōl eṉpatu kiṭuku paṭi allatu parici eṉappaṭum. kēṭayam eṉpatum atu. pala vīrarkaḷum oruṅkē tōl piṭittup pōrkkaḷattu niṟṟalip paḵṟōl eṉpar. ataṉi maḻi mukilukku oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭutal peruvaḻakku.
maḻimaruḷ paḵṟōl	- malipaṭu. 377
paḵṟōl pōlac ceṉmaḻi	- naṟ. 197
maḻiyeṉa maruḷum paḵṟōl	- puṟam. 17
eṉpavaṟṟik kāṇka!
pallaṭiyākak kiḷitta coṟkaḷ ivitāmō? iṉṉum palavākalām. aṟiñarkaḷ eṇṇuvārkaḷāka.
palkutal:
palkutal = palavākap perukutal.
oru kuṭumpattu makkaḷ ōrūrāka viritalum ōrāṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷ oru mantiyāka viritalum palkutalām. palavakiyākap perukutal palkip perukutal eṉappaṭum. palkutal, palukutal eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. orē ōr āṭu vāṅkiṉōm; naṉṟākap paluki nālāṇṭil nāṟpatu uruppaṭikku mēl ākiviṭṭatu eṉpatil palukutal eṉpatu perukutal poruḷil varutal aṟiyalām. perukutal kūṭutaltāṉē. vīṭellām eli palukiviṭṭatu; pūṉi vaḷarttāltāṉ carippaṭum eṉpatum vaḻakkē. āṭu māṭukaḷ karukkoḷḷal palappaṭutal eṉṟum, kuṭṭi, kaṉṟu pōṭutal palukutal eṉṟum, pūvil palaṉ piṭittal eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkukaḷ oppiṭṭaṟiyattakkaṉa.
palcanta māli:
pal cantaṅkaḷāl, pattu mutal nūṟu pāṭalkaḷ varuvatu palcanta māliyākum. pala cantam eṉparāyiṉum pattuc cantaṅkaḷ varutal vaḻakku eṉka.
cantam eṉpatu nāṉkeḻuttu mutal iruppattāṟu eḻuttaḷavum orō aṭiyāṉ ottu varuvatu eṉka.
pattuk koru cantam pāṭippā nūṟāka
vittaltāṉ palcanta māliyām	- pira. tira.
pattu mutalākap pappat tīṟā
vitta vaṇṇa vakipat tākap
palcanta māli pakarap paṭumē	- ilak. pāṭ. 74
palpeyar:
oṉṟaṟkuriya pala peyarkaḷiyum muṟiyē kūṟum pāṭal tokuti palpeyar eṉappaṭum. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu, ñāṉa campantar pāṭiya palpeyarp pattu ākum.
piramapuram, vēṇupuram, pūmpukali, veṅkuru, tōṇipuram, pūntarāy, civapuram, puṟavam, caṇpi, cīrkāḻi, kocci, kaḻumalam eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muṟiyē varappāṭappaṭṭuḷḷa patikam palpeyarp pattām.
oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi vaḷarcci ippatikap paṉuval (ōriṭappaṉmoḻi) eṉattakum.
pallam:
palavakik kāykaḷip pōṭṭu vikkat takkatākavum akaṉṟatākavum taṭuppukaḷ aminta kaṭṭukkoṭip piṉṉal taṭṭukaḷip pallam eṉpatu eḻumali vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. aintaṟip peṭṭiyip pōlap palavakik kāykaḷit taṉittaṉiyē pōṭṭu vikka amintatu itu. pala taṭṭu (taṭuppu) uṭiyatu pallam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pallāṅkuḻi:
patiṉāṉku kuḻi > paṉṉāṅkuḻi > pallāṅkuḻi.
patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭṭiyāl allatu māḻiyāl (ulōkattāl) aminta caturap palaki itu.
tirumaṇa vīṭukaḷil peritum makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭāka - makiḻvāṉa - poḻutupōkkāka amintatu ivviḷiyāṭṭu. muṟkālattil celvarkaḷ maṉṉarkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭākavum iruntuḷatu. vaṭṭāṭutalil (cūtāṭalil) cūtum vātum uṇṭu. itil avi illi. palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum ivviḷiyāṭṭu āṭappaṭukiṟatu.
kuḻikaḷukku vālkuḻi, mūlikkuḻi, kācikkuḻi, poykkuḻi eṉappeyarkaḷ uṇṭu.
pallāṭal:
pallāṭal = uṇṇal.
viṭintatil iruntu innēram vari pallāṭavē illi eṉiṉ uṇṇavilli eṉpatu poruḷām. pallāṭutal ākiya pallacivu pal maruttuvarit tēṭutaṟ kuriyatu. uṇṇutaṟku aci pōṭutal eṉpatoru peyar. acittal eṉpatum uṇṇutalē. palli acikka mella - vakiyiṉṟi iruppatu uṇṇā vaṟumiyi urippatām. pallil paccit taṇṇīr paṭavilli eṉpatum attakiyatoru vaḻakkē.
pallāṇṭu:
pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭu pallāyirattāṇṭu
palakōṭi nūṟāyiram
mallāṇṭa tiṇtōḷ maṇivaṇṇā uṉ
cevvaṭi cevvi tirukkāppu
eṉak kuṟaḷ veṇ centuṟiyāl toṭaṅkum pallāṇṭu aṟucīr āciriya viruttam patiṉoṉṟāl niṟikiṉṟatu. itu periyāḻvār pāṭiyatu.
maṉṉari vāḻttutal paḻamiyāṉatu. puṟappāṭalil puravalari vāḻttik kūṟiya vāḻttukaḷ mikappala. avi akaval naṭiyaṉa.
iṟivaṉi vāḻttutal uṇṭu; akaval, kaliveṇpā, viruttam iṉṉa pala yāppukaḷil pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum, pallāṇṭu eṉṉum collāṭciyum, pāṭaltōṟum pallāṇṭu kūṟutum eṉṉum muṭiniliyum āḻvār aruḷiyaviyē.
viyaka maṉṉavaṉ maṉṉuka palyāṇ
ṭeytuka veṉpa tiyāṇṭunili yākum
eṉpatu paṉṉirupāṭṭiyal (328).
maṉṉavaṉ mālōṭoppāṉ eṉṟamiyāl mālukkup pallāṇṭu pāṭiṉār pōlum āḻvār.
palli:
pal + i = palli. taṉikkuṟil muṉ vanta mey ataṟku muṉ uyir eḻuttu vara iraṇṭākum eṉṉum vitippaṭi pal + l + i = palli yāyatu. palliyiṉ nīḷattiyum parumiyiyum pārttu avaṟṟuṭaṉ palliṉ parumi nīḷam ākiyavaṟṟiyum oppiṭṭuk kaṇṭāl evvaḷavu uṉṉippākap pārttu ippeyariṭṭuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum; viyappum tōṉṟum. pallaṉ pallāyi eṉṉum paṭṭap peyar vaḻakkum nōkkuka.
palluccōḷam:
cōḷa vakiyuḷ oṉṟu veḷḷic cōḷam. ataṉi muttuc cōḷam eṉpatu potuvaḻakku. ataṉ veṇṇiṟam kuṟittup palluccōḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkutal kaṇṭamaṉūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum.
pallik kāṭṭal:
pallik kāṭṭal = keñcutal, cirittal.
eṇṇāṉku muppattiraṇṭu paṟkaḷiyum tiṟantu kāṭṭi eṉa oru pulavar pāṭiṉār. pallik kāṭṭal keñcutal poruḷil varuvatām.
pallik kāṭṭutal vāyit tiṟantu koṇṭiruttal, cirittal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiyum tarum. colvatik kēḷāmal pallik kāṭṭik koṇṭirukkiṟāyē eṉak kaṭivatu uṇṭu.
evvaḷavu maṟuttālum, veṟuttālum taṉ vēṇṭu kiyi viṭāmal collik koṇṭiruppatē keñcutal poruḷ tarum pallik kāṭṭutalām.
pallip piṭittup pārttal:
pallip piṭittup pārttal = ārāytal.
māṭu piṭippār māṭṭiṉ akaviyit terivataṟkup pallip piṭittup pārppar. avvakiyāl pallip piṭittal ārāytal poruḷ peṟṟatu. ataṉāl oruvarip paṟṟi oruvar maṟṟoruvariṭam palappalavaṟṟik kēṭṭu aṟintālum, nēril viṉaviyaṟiyat talippaṭṭālum eṉṉiyē pallip piṭittup pārkkiṟāṉ? eṉa ikaḻvatu uṇṭu.
ilavacamākak koṭutta māṭṭip pallip piṭittup pārttāṉām eṉa varum paḻamoḻi, viliyillāp poruḷi ettakiyatāyiṉum ōraḷavu payaṉpaṭumeṉiṉum koḷka eṉṉum poruḷil varuvatām.
